<script id="ethvelle-text-section-update-v22-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const body=document.body;
  const app=document.getElementById('app');
  if(!body||!app)return;
  let locked=false;
  let originalHeight='',originalHeightPriority='',originalMinHeight='',originalMinHeightPriority='';
  function restoreProperty(el,name,value,priority){
    if(value)el.style.setProperty(name,value,priority||'');
    else el.style.removeProperty(name);
  }
  function syncComposerViewportLock(){
    const shouldLock=body.classList.contains('evtc-is-open');
    if(shouldLock&&!locked){
      locked=true;
      originalHeight=app.style.getPropertyValue('height');
      originalHeightPriority=app.style.getPropertyPriority('height');
      originalMinHeight=app.style.getPropertyValue('min-height');
      originalMinHeightPriority=app.style.getPropertyPriority('min-height');
      const height=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(window.innerHeight||document.documentElement.clientHeight||600));
      app.style.setProperty('height',height+'px','important');
      app.style.setProperty('min-height',height+'px','important');
    }else if(!shouldLock&&locked){
      restoreProperty(app,'height',originalHeight,originalHeightPriority);
      restoreProperty(app,'min-height',originalMinHeight,originalMinHeightPriority);
      locked=false;
    }
  }
  const classObserver=new MutationObserver(syncComposerViewportLock);
  classObserver.observe(body,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  syncComposerViewportLock();
  // If device orientation/window size changes before the keyboard takes focus, refresh the
  // locked height. While typing, leave it untouched so the keyboard cannot shrink the editor.
  window.addEventListener('resize',function(){
    if(!locked)return;
    const input=document.getElementById('evtcStageText');
    if(input&&document.activeElement===input)return;
    const height=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(window.innerHeight||document.documentElement.clientHeight||600));
    app.style.setProperty('height',height+'px','important');
    app.style.setProperty('min-height',height+'px','important');
  },{passive:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-ux-v27-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* Reliable single-tap activation for the bottom text-tool tabs on mobile WebViews. */
  function bindTabTap(){
    const tabs=$('evpaTabs');
    if(!tabs||tabs.dataset.evV27TapBound==='1')return;
    tabs.dataset.evV27TapBound='1';
    tabs.addEventListener('click',function(e){
      const tab=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('.evpa-tab[data-tab]'):null;
      if(!tab||!tabs.contains(tab))return;
      const tool=String(tab.dataset.tab||'');
      if(!tool||typeof window.ethvellePicsartSetTool!=='function')return;
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      try{window.ethvellePicsartSetTool(tool);}catch(err){console.error('Text tool tab activation failed',err);return;}
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
        if(!dock)return;
        const noPalette=['spacing','opacity','styles','perspective','bend','format','gradient','texture'].includes(tool);
        dock.classList.toggle('evpa-no-palette',noPalette);
        tabs.querySelectorAll('.evpa-tab[data-tab]').forEach(b=>{
          const active=b.dataset.tab===tool;
          b.classList.toggle('active',active);
          b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');
        });
        const palette=$('evpaPalette');
        if(palette)palette.setAttribute('aria-hidden',noPalette?'true':'false');
        const head=dock.querySelector('.evpa-sheet-title');
        if(head)head.textContent=tab.querySelector('span')?.textContent||tool;
      });
    },true);
  }

  /* Re-apply background fills to each transformed character when text is bent. Painting the
     gradient/texture on the full untransformed word leaves the fill straight while letters bend.
     A per-character clipped background shares the same word-sized background and offset so its
     color/pattern travels with each character transform. */
  let renderQueued=false;
  function queueBendTextureSync(){
    if(renderQueued)return;
    renderQueued=true;
    const schedule=window.requestAnimationFrame||function(fn){return window.setTimeout(fn,16);};
    schedule(()=>{renderQueued=false;syncBendTextureFills();});
  }
  function resolveBackgroundSize(size,width,height){
    const raw=String(size||'100% 100%').trim();
    if(raw==='cover'||raw==='contain')return `${Math.max(1,width)}px ${Math.max(1,height)}px`;
    const parts=raw.split(/\s+/);
    const resolve=(v,dim)=>{
      if(!v||v==='auto')return `${Math.max(1,dim)}px`;
      if(v.endsWith('%'))return `${Math.max(1,parseFloat(v)*dim/100)}px`;
      return v;
    };
    return `${resolve(parts[0],width)} ${resolve(parts[1]||'auto',height)}`;
  }
  function syncBendTextureFills(){
    const root=$('overlayMediaLayer');
    if(!root)return;
    let list=[];
    try{if(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))list=overlays;else if(Array.isArray(window.overlays))list=window.overlays;}catch(_){list=[];}
    root.querySelectorAll('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content').forEach(node=>{
      const glyph=node.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
      if(!glyph)return;
      /* v306: the text host only carries data-ev-mask-owner-id (no data-overlay-id), so the layer was never found,
         the per-letter gradient was never painted and the bent text stayed invisible (transparent fill). */
      const host=node.closest('[data-overlay-id],[data-ov-id],[data-ev-mask-owner-id]');
      const id=host?(host.dataset.overlayId||host.dataset.ovId||host.dataset.evMaskOwnerId):'';
      let t=list.find(item=>item&&item.type==='text'&&String(item.id)===String(id));
      if(!t){try{t=list.find(item=>item&&item.type==='text'&&typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[item.id]&&overlayMediaEls[item.id].contains(node));}catch(_e){}}
      const fill=t?String(t.textGradient||t.textTexture||''):'';
      const bent=!!glyph.dataset.evpaBendRaw&&glyph.children.length>0;
      const chars=Array.from(glyph.children).filter(el=>el.tagName==='SPAN');
      if(!fill||!bent||!chars.length){
        chars.forEach(span=>{
          span.style.removeProperty('background-image');
          span.style.removeProperty('background-clip');
          span.style.removeProperty('-webkit-background-clip');
          span.style.removeProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color');
          span.style.removeProperty('background-size');
          span.style.removeProperty('background-position');
          span.style.removeProperty('background-repeat');
        });
        return;
      }
      const computed=getComputedStyle(glyph);
      const width=Math.max(1,glyph.offsetWidth||node.offsetWidth||1);
      const height=Math.max(1,glyph.offsetHeight||node.offsetHeight||1);
      const size=resolveBackgroundSize(computed.backgroundSize||glyph.style.backgroundSize,width,height);
      const repeat=computed.backgroundRepeat||'repeat';
      let fallbackLeft=0;
      chars.forEach(span=>{
        const left=Number.isFinite(span.offsetLeft)?span.offsetLeft:fallbackLeft;
        const top=Number.isFinite(span.offsetTop)?span.offsetTop:0;
        span.style.backgroundImage=fill;
        span.style.backgroundClip='text';
        span.style.webkitBackgroundClip='text';
        span.style.webkitTextFillColor='transparent';
        span.style.backgroundSize=size;
        span.style.backgroundPosition=`-${left}px -${top}px`;
        span.style.backgroundRepeat=repeat;
        span.style.backgroundOrigin='border-box';
        fallbackLeft+=span.offsetWidth||0;
      });
      glyph.style.backgroundImage='none';
      glyph.style.backgroundClip='border-box';
      glyph.style.webkitBackgroundClip='border-box';
      glyph.style.webkitTextFillColor='';
    });
  }
  function wrapRender(name){
    const prior=window[name];
    if(typeof prior!=='function'||prior.__evV27BendFill)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=prior.apply(this,arguments);if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing)queueBendTextureSync();return result;};
    wrapped.__evV27BendFill=true;
    window[name]=wrapped;
  }
  window.ethvelleSyncBendFillsNow=function(){try{syncBendTextureFills();}catch(_e){}};
  function boot(){
    bindTabTap();
    wrapRender('renderFrame');
    wrapRender('renderOverlayLayers');
    queueBendTextureSync();
    const observerRoot=$('overlayMediaLayer');
    if(observerRoot&&!observerRoot.dataset.evV27TextureObserver){
      observerRoot.dataset.evV27TextureObserver='1';
      new MutationObserver(queueBendTextureSync).observe(observerRoot,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    }
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  const dockObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>bindTabTap());
  if(document.body)dockObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

  }

    #ethvellePicsartTextDock #evpaPalette .evpa-swatches,

      grid-template-columns:repeat(6,35px)!important;
      gap:7px 8px!important;

    }

  /* Keep the text's white canvas transform frame crisp when the selected item is a split segment. */
  #selBoxLayer.ethvelle-text-selected-box .sb-frame {
    display:block!important;

<script id="ethvelle-text-editing-repair-v29-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  function allTextOverlays(){
    try{return (typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:(window.overlays||[])).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text');}
    catch(_e){return (window.overlays||[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text');}
  }
  function selectedId(){
    try{return String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'?(window.selectedClip??''):(selectedClip??''));}
    catch(_e){return String(window.selectedClip??'');}
  }
  function rgbaFromHex(hex,opacity){
    let h=String(hex||'#000000').trim().replace(/^#/,'');
    if(/^[0-9a-f]{3}$/i.test(h))h=h.split('').map(c=>c+c).join('');
    if(!/^[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h))h='000000';
    const a=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(opacity==null?100:opacity)))/100;
    return `rgba(${parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)},${a})`;
  }
  function getTextNode(ov){
    if(!ov)return null;
    let host=null;
    try{host=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;}catch(_e){}
    if(!host){const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');if(layer)host=Array.from(layer.children).find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId||n.dataset.ovId||'')===String(ov.id))||null;}
    return host?.querySelector?.('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content')||null;
  }
  // CSS text-shadow paints behind the glyph. A positive, visible Y offset prevents it from
  // reading like a dark fill over the letters; X=0 is preserved instead of being replaced by 4.
  function applyDownwardTextShadows(){
    allTextOverlays().forEach(ov=>{
      const node=getTextNode(ov);if(!node)return;
      const glyph=node.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
      const x=Number.isFinite(Number(ov.shadowX))?Number(ov.shadowX):0;
      const rawY=Number(ov.shadowY);
      const y=Math.max(4,Number.isFinite(rawY)?rawY:6);
      const blur=Math.max(0,Number.isFinite(Number(ov.shadowBlur))?Number(ov.shadowBlur):4);
      const shadow=ov.shadowEnabled?`${x}px ${y}px ${blur}px ${rgbaFromHex(ov.shadowColor||'#000000',ov.shadowOpacity)}`:'none';
      if(glyph){
        if(node.dataset.evV29ShadowNode!=='none'){node.style.setProperty('text-shadow','none','important');node.dataset.evV29ShadowNode='none';}
        const _evFill=!!(String(ov.textGradient||'').trim()||String(ov.textTexture||'').trim()),_evKey=shadow+(_evFill?'|fill':'');
        if(glyph.dataset.evV29Shadow!==_evKey){window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow(glyph,shadow,_evFill,true);glyph.dataset.evV29Shadow=_evKey;}
      }else if(node.dataset.evV29Shadow!==shadow){
        node.style.setProperty('text-shadow',shadow,'important');node.dataset.evV29Shadow=shadow;
      }
    });
  }
  // Re-apply after every canvas refresh so legacy renderers cannot put the shadow back on the wrapper.
  const oldRenderFrame=window.renderFrame;
  if(typeof oldRenderFrame==='function'&&!oldRenderFrame.__ethvelleV29Shadow){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldRenderFrame.apply(this,arguments);if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing)applyDownwardTextShadows();return result;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV29Shadow=true;window.renderFrame=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSetColor=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof oldSetColor==='function'&&!oldSetColor.__ethvelleV29Shadow){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSetColor.apply(this,arguments);applyDownwardTextShadows();return result;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV29Shadow=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrapped;
  }
  // Selecting a split text segment is selection-only. The previous workaround nudged the
  // playhead inside the split segment and rendered a frame whenever selection was outside it.
  // That made switching layers unexpectedly seek the timeline and could trigger a heavy media
  // render while the user was still interacting. Keep the selected text's style/frame updates,
  // but never change absolute time as a side effect of selecting it.
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__ethvelleV29SplitFrame){
    const wrapped=function(which){
      const result=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);
      const id=String(which??selectedId());
      const ov=allTextOverlays().find(o=>String(o.id)===id);
      if(!ov)return result;
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
        if(selectedId()!==String(ov.id))return;
        applyDownwardTextShadows();
        try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
        const layer=$('selBoxLayer');
        if(layer&&selectedId()===String(ov.id))layer.classList.add('ethvelle-text-selected-box');
      });
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV29SplitFrame=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  // Keep the frame in sync when a repaint creates a split segment's DOM text node after selection.
  const oldUpdateSelBox=window.updateSelBox;
  if(typeof oldUpdateSelBox==='function'&&!oldUpdateSelBox.__ethvelleV29Frame){
    const wrapped=function(){const out=oldUpdateSelBox.apply(this,arguments);const ov=allTextOverlays().find(o=>String(o.id)===selectedId());const layer=$('selBoxLayer');if(layer)layer.classList.toggle('ethvelle-text-selected-box',!!ov);return out;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV29Frame=true;window.updateSelBox=wrapped;
  }
  function boot(){applyDownwardTextShadows();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-text-section-update-v28-js">
(function(){
  function syncSliderColorMode(){
    const dock=document.getElementById('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    const palette=document.getElementById('evpaPalette');
    const tabs=document.getElementById('evpaTabs');
    if(!dock||!palette||!tabs)return;
    const active=tabs.querySelector('.evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';
    const controls=document.getElementById('evpaControls')||dock.querySelector('.evpa-controls');
    const hasSlider=!!(controls&&controls.querySelector('input[type="range"]'));
    const paletteVisible=!dock.classList.contains('evpa-no-palette')&&active!=='font';
    dock.classList.toggle('evpa-slider-color-mode',hasSlider&&paletteVisible);
  }
  function boot(){
    const tabs=document.getElementById('evpaTabs');
    if(tabs&&!tabs.dataset.evV28Mode){tabs.dataset.evV28Mode='1';tabs.addEventListener('click',()=>requestAnimationFrame(syncSliderColorMode),true);}
    const dock=document.getElementById('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock){new MutationObserver(syncSliderColorMode).observe(dock,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});}
    syncSliderColorMode();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-editor-repair-v30-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  let layoutQueued=false;
  function activeDockTab(dock){return dock?.querySelector('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';}
  function isColorGridActive(dock){return !!dock && !dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer') && !dock.classList.contains('evpa-no-palette') && activeDockTab(dock)==='color';}
  function restoreOriginalSwatchOrder(swatches){
    const utilities=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-utilities');
    const grid=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-grid');
    if(!utilities&&!grid)return;
    const nodes=[...(utilities?[...utilities.children]:[]),...(grid?[...grid.children]:[])];
    nodes.sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.dataset.evpaColorOrder)||0)-(Number(b.dataset.evpaColorOrder)||0));
    nodes.forEach(node=>{delete node.dataset.evpaColorOrder;swatches.appendChild(node);});
    utilities?.remove();grid?.remove();
  }
  function arrangeColorSwatches(swatches){
    if(swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-utilities')||swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-grid'))return;
    const nodes=[...swatches.children];
    if(!nodes.length)return;
    nodes.forEach((node,index)=>node.dataset.evpaColorOrder=String(index));
    const utilities=document.createElement('div');utilities.className='evpa-color-utilities';utilities.setAttribute('aria-label','Color tools');
    const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='evpa-color-grid';grid.setAttribute('aria-label','Color swatches');
    nodes.forEach(node=>{if(node.classList.contains('evpa-swatch'))grid.appendChild(node);else utilities.appendChild(node);});
    swatches.append(utilities,grid);
  }
  function syncColorLayout(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;
    const colorMode=isColorGridActive(dock);
    const tab=activeDockTab(dock);
    const bottomPaletteMode=!dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer') && !dock.classList.contains('evpa-no-palette') && ['highlight','stroke','shadow'].includes(tab);
    dock.classList.toggle('evpa-color-grid-mode',colorMode);
    dock.classList.toggle('evpa-bottom-palette-mode',bottomPaletteMode);
    const palette=$('evpaPalette');if(!palette)return;
    palette.querySelectorAll('.evpa-swatches').forEach(swatches=>{
      if(colorMode)arrangeColorSwatches(swatches);else restoreOriginalSwatchOrder(swatches);
    });
  }
  function queueSync(){if(layoutQueued)return;layoutQueued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{layoutQueued=false;syncColorLayout();});}
  function boot(){
    syncColorLayout();
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock&&!dock.dataset.evV30LayoutObserver){
      dock.dataset.evV30LayoutObserver='1';
      new MutationObserver(queueSync).observe(dock,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});
    }
    const tabs=$('evpaTabs');
    if(tabs&&!tabs.dataset.evV30ColorTap){tabs.dataset.evV30ColorTap='1';tabs.addEventListener('click',queueSync,true);}
    // Final frame refresh after the target canvas handler has applied each movement step.
    const canvas=$('canvasStage')||$('stickCanvas');
    if(canvas&&!canvas.dataset.evV30FrameFollow){
      canvas.dataset.evV30FrameFollow='1';
      const refreshFrame=()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}});
      // canvasStage contains both the drawing canvas and DOM text/image overlays, so the
      // selection frame is refreshed even while a finger is over a rendered overlay layer.
      canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',refreshFrame,{passive:true});
      canvas.addEventListener('pointerup',refreshFrame,{passive:true});
      canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel',refreshFrame,{passive:true});
    }
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  const bodyObserver=new MutationObserver(queueSync);
  if(document.body)bodyObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

    overflow-x:hidden!important;overflow-y:auto!important;touch-action:pan-y!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-color-grid-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) #evpaPalette .evpa-color-grid>.evpa-swatch {

    height:35px!important;min-height:35px!important;max-height:35px!important;margin:0!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-color-grid-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) #evpaPalette .evpa-color-grid {order:1!important;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-color-grid-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) #evpaPalette .evpa-color-utilities {
    order:2!important;flex:0 0 35px!important;height:35px!important;min-height:35px!important;max-height:35px!important;
    margin:8px 0 0!important;padding:0!important;display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:flex-start!important;gap:8px!important;
    background:transparent!important;border:0!important;outline:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-color-grid-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) #evpaPalette .evpa-color-utilities>button {
    background-color:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;outline:0!important;
  }

  /* The text wrapper/backplate must stay untransformed; only the glyph child gets effects. */
  #overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content.ev-text-highlight-plate {transform:none!important;filter:none!important;}
  #overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content.ev-text-highlight-plate::before {
    transform:none!important;filter:none!important;box-shadow:none!important;outline:none!important;
  }
  #overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content>.evpa-text-effect-layer {
    display:inline-block!important;position:relative;transform-origin:center center!important;transform-style:preserve-3d;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-text-section-update-v32-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const raf=fn=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{fn();}catch(_e){}});
  const safeSelectedId=()=>{try{return String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?selectedClip:(window.selectedClip??''));}catch(_e){return String(window.selectedClip??'');}};
  const allOverlays=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:(window.overlays||[]);}catch(_e){return window.overlays||[];}};
  const allBackgrounds=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:(window.backgrounds||[]);}catch(_e){return window.backgrounds||[];}};
  function activeTab(dock){return dock?.querySelector('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';}

  /* Rehouse Font-tab tools into a compact action row so 8 actual colour swatches fit on-screen. */
  function restoreFontPalette(swatches){
    if(!swatches)return;
    const tools=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-tools');
    const row=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-row');
    if(!tools&&!row)return;
    const nodes=[...(tools?[...tools.children]:[]),...(row?[...row.children]:[])];
    nodes.sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.dataset.evpaFontOrder)||0)-(Number(b.dataset.evpaFontOrder)||0));
    nodes.forEach(el=>{delete el.dataset.evpaFontOrder;swatches.appendChild(el);});
    tools?.remove();row?.remove();
  }
  function layoutFontPalette(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;
    const fontMode=!dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')&&activeTab(dock)==='font';
    dock.classList.toggle('evpa-font-palette-mode',fontMode);
    const swatches=$('evpaPalette')?.querySelector('.evpa-swatches');if(!swatches)return;
    if(!fontMode){restoreFontPalette(swatches);return;}
    if(swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-row'))return;
    const children=[...swatches.children];if(!children.length)return;
    children.forEach((el,index)=>el.dataset.evpaFontOrder=String(index));
    const tools=children.filter(el=>!el.classList.contains('evpa-swatch'));
    const colours=children.filter(el=>el.classList.contains('evpa-swatch'));
    const toolsRow=document.createElement('div');toolsRow.className='evpa-font-color-tools';toolsRow.setAttribute('aria-label','Colour actions');
    const colourRow=document.createElement('div');colourRow.className='evpa-font-color-row';colourRow.setAttribute('aria-label','Text colours; swipe left or right');
    tools.forEach(el=>toolsRow.appendChild(el));colours.forEach(el=>colourRow.appendChild(el));
    swatches.replaceChildren(toolsRow,colourRow);
  }
  let paletteQueued=false;
  function queuePaletteLayout(){if(paletteQueued)return;paletteQueued=true;raf(()=>{paletteQueued=false;layoutFontPalette();});}

  /* Keep visual effects on glyph paint only. The background/highlight is an independent backplate. */
  function rgba(hex,opacity){
    let h=String(hex||'#000000').trim().replace(/^#/,'');if(/^[0-9a-f]{3}$/i.test(h))h=h.split('').map(c=>c+c).join('');
    if(!/^[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h))h='000000';const a=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(opacity==null?100:opacity)))/100;
    return `rgba(${parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)},${parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)},${a})`;
  }
  function glyphFor(node){
    let glyph=node.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
    if(!glyph){glyph=document.createElement('span');glyph.className='evpa-text-effect-layer';while(node.firstChild)glyph.appendChild(node.firstChild);node.appendChild(glyph);}
    return glyph;
  }
  function syncGlyphEffects(){
    const list=allOverlays();
    list.filter(ov=>ov&&ov.type==='text').forEach(ov=>{
      let host=null;try{host=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;}catch(_e){}
      if(!host){const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');if(layer)host=[...layer.children].find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId||n.dataset.ovId||'')===String(ov.id))||null;}
      const node=host?.querySelector?.('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content');if(!node)return;
      const glyph=glyphFor(node);
      const px=Number(ov.textPerspectiveX)||0,py=Number(ov.textPerspectiveY)||0;
      node.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');
      node.style.setProperty('filter','none','important');
      glyph.style.setProperty('transform',(px||py)?`perspective(700px) rotateX(${py}deg) rotateY(${px}deg)`:'none','important');
      glyph.style.setProperty('transform-origin','center center','important');
      glyph.style.setProperty('transform-style','preserve-3d','important');
      window.ethvelleApplyTextShadow(glyph,ov.shadowEnabled?`${Number.isFinite(Number(ov.shadowX))?Number(ov.shadowX):0}px ${Math.max(4,Number.isFinite(Number(ov.shadowY))?Number(ov.shadowY):6)}px ${Math.max(0,Number.isFinite(Number(ov.shadowBlur))?Number(ov.shadowBlur):4)}px ${rgba(ov.shadowColor||'#000000',ov.shadowOpacity)}`:'none',!!(String(ov.textGradient||'').trim()||String(ov.textTexture||'').trim()),true);
      glyph.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-stroke',Number(ov.strokeWidth)>0?`${Number(ov.strokeWidth)}px ${rgba(ov.strokeColor||'#000000',ov.strokeOpacity)}`:'0 transparent','important');
      node.style.setProperty('text-shadow','none','important');
      node.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-stroke','0 transparent','important');
      /* Do not let any legacy wrapper styles cast a shadow or transform onto the backplate. */
      if(ov.textBackgroundEnabled||ov.textHighlightEnabled){
        node.classList.add('ev-text-highlight-plate');
        node.style.setProperty('background-color','transparent','important');
        node.style.setProperty('background-image','none','important');
      }
    });
  }
  function wrapRender(name){
    const prior=window[name];
    if(typeof prior!=='function'||prior.__evV32EffectsAndSelection)return;
    // renderFrame is the high-frequency path during playback/scrubbing. A full scan of every
    // clip and a palette DOM rebuild after each frame was needlessly expensive on Android.
    if(name==='renderFrame')return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=prior.apply(this,arguments);
      if(name==='renderOverlayMedia'){
        // Text effects are static during a scrub. Reapply them once on release rather than
        // on every frame; overlay creation/removal is separately observed below.
        if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing)raf(()=>{if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing)syncGlyphEffects();});
        return result;
      }
      // These are structural redraws, not per-frame preview ticks. Selection sync stays here;
      // palette layout is triggered by palette events/observers, never by timeline playback.
      raf(()=>{
        if(name==='renderOverlayLayers')syncGlyphEffects();
        if(name==='renderOverlayLayers'||name==='renderTimelineClip'||name==='renderBackgroundTrack')syncTimelineSelection();
      });
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV32EffectsAndSelection=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  }

  /* Retain the selection on whichever timeline clip is under a user's slide, across all tracks. */
  function setSelectedClassForAll(){
    const id=safeSelectedId();
    const rawOwner=window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex;
    const ownerIndex=rawOwner==null?null:Number(rawOwner);
    const hasOwner=Number.isInteger(ownerIndex)&&ownerIndex>=0;
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .anim-clip').forEach(el=>{
      const key=el.id==='animClipEl'?'main':(el.dataset.segIdx!=null?'seg'+el.dataset.segIdx:(el.dataset.readonlyClipRef!=null?String(el.dataset.readonlyClipRef):''));
      const row=el.closest('.char-row');
      const rowIndex=row&&row.dataset.charIdx!=null?Number(row.dataset.charIdx):null;
      const ownerMatches=!hasOwner||rowIndex==null||rowIndex===ownerIndex;
      if(key)el.classList.toggle('selected',key===id&&ownerMatches);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('selected',String(el.dataset.overlayId)===id));
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .track-clip[data-bg-id]').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('selected',String(el.dataset.bgId)===id));
    document.querySelectorAll('#timelineInner .char-row').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('chrome-selected','char'+el.dataset.charIdx===id));
  }
  function setLightTimelineSelection(id,characterIndex=null){
    if(id==null||String(id)==='')return false;
    const key=String(id);
    const owner=characterIndex==null?null:(Number.isInteger(Number(characterIndex))?Number(characterIndex):null);
    const pending=window.__ethvelleTimelinePendingSelection;
    if(String(safeSelectedId())===key&&window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex===owner&&pending&&String(pending.id)===key)return false;
    selectedClip=id;
    selectedKfIdx=null;
    window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
    window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex=owner;
    window.__ethvelleTimelinePendingSelection={id,characterIndex:owner};
    setSelectedClassForAll();
    try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
    const ovNow=selectedOverlay(key);
    const box=$('selBoxLayer');
    if(box)box.classList.toggle('ethvelle-text-selected-box',!!ovNow&&ovNow.type==='text');
    return true;
  }
  function commitLightTimelineSelection(){
    const pending=window.__ethvelleTimelinePendingSelection;
    if(!pending)return false;
    window.__ethvelleTimelinePendingSelection=null;
    const owner=pending.characterIndex;
    try{
      if(owner!=null&&Number.isInteger(Number(owner))&&Number(owner)!==Number(activeCharIdx)&&typeof window.selectCharacter==='function')window.selectCharacter(Number(owner));
      if(typeof window.selectClipForChrome==='function')window.selectClipForChrome(pending.id);
    }catch(_e){}
    return true;
  }
  window.__ethvelleLightSelectTimelineClip=setLightTimelineSelection;
  window.__ethvelleCommitTimelineSelection=commitLightTimelineSelection;
  function selectedOverlay(id){return allOverlays().find(o=>String(o?.id)===String(id))||null;}
  function focusFrameOnSelected(id){
    // Selection chrome is visual only. Selecting a clip must never seek the playhead,
    // render a new frame, or move timelineScroll; taps have their own explicit seek path.
    raf(()=>{
      if(safeSelectedId()!==String(id))return;
      setSelectedClassForAll();
      try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
      const ovNow=selectedOverlay(safeSelectedId());
      const box=$('selBoxLayer');
      if(box)box.classList.toggle('ethvelle-text-selected-box',!!ovNow&&ovNow.type==='text');
    });
  }
  function syncTimelineSelection(){setSelectedClassForAll();}
  const priorSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof priorSelect==='function'&&!priorSelect.__evV32FollowSelection){
    const wrapped=function(which){
      const result=priorSelect.apply(this,arguments);
      const id=String(which??safeSelectedId());
      window.__ethvelleTimelinePendingSelection=null;
      window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex=(id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id))?Number(activeCharIdx):null;
      raf(()=>{if(safeSelectedId()===id)focusFrameOnSelected(id);});
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV32FollowSelection=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }

  function wireSlideSelection(){
    const scroller=$('timelineScroll');if(!scroller||scroller.dataset.evV32SlideSelection)return;
    scroller.dataset.evV32SlideSelection='1';let tracking=null;
    const targetAt=(x,y)=>{
      const el=document.elementFromPoint(x,y);if(!el)return null;
      let target=null;try{target=typeof timelineClipTargetAt==='function'?timelineClipTargetAt(el):null;}catch(_e){}
      if(!target){
        const readonly=el.closest&&el.closest('.anim-clip-readonly[data-readonly-clip-ref]');
        if(readonly)return String(readonly.dataset.readonlyClipRef);
      }
      if(!target)return null;
      return target.type==='main'?'main':target.type==='seg'?'seg'+target.index:String(target.model?.id??'');
    };
    scroller.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(e.button>0)return;
      const id=targetAt(e.clientX,e.clientY);if(!id)return;
      tracking={pointerId:e.pointerId,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,lastId:id,moved:false};
      const startRow=(document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX,e.clientY)||e.target).closest?.('.overlay-track-row,#backgroundRow,.char-row')||null;
      window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer={pointerId:e.pointerId,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,active:true,moved:false,startRow};
    },{capture:true,passive:true});
    // Track only the live finger coordinates here. v76 owns the single RAF-coalesced
    // trimStart/trimEnd lookup and light selection update, triggered by the scrub's
    // setCurrentAbsoluteTime wrapper. This avoids doing the same model scan twice per frame.
    scroller.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      const d=tracking;if(!d||d.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
      if(Math.hypot(e.clientX-d.startX,e.clientY-d.startY)>=7)d.moved=true;
      if(!d.moved)return;
      const prevRow=window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer&&window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer.startRow;
      window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer={pointerId:e.pointerId,x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY,active:true,moved:true,startRow:prevRow};
    },{passive:true});
    const finish=e=>{
      if(!tracking||tracking.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
      const d=tracking;
      tracking=null;
      // Keep the last row/pointer coordinates available until v76's single pointer-up
      // RAF resolves the final clip and commits its heavy selection synchronization.
      if(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer&&window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer.pointerId===e.pointerId)window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer.ending=true;
    };
    scroller.addEventListener('pointerup',finish,{capture:true,passive:true});
    scroller.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{
      if(tracking&&tracking.pointerId===e.pointerId)tracking=null;
      if(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer&&window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer.pointerId===e.pointerId)window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer.active=false;
    },{capture:true,passive:true});
  }

  function wireClipTapSeek(){
    const scroller=$('timelineScroll');if(!scroller||scroller.dataset.evV32ClipTapSeek)return;
    scroller.dataset.evV32ClipTapSeek='1';
    let tap=null;
    const clipSelector='.anim-clip,.overlay-clip,.track-clip[data-bg-id]';
    const excludedSelector='.kf-marker,.anim-clip-handle,[data-ov-handle],[data-seg-handle],[data-bg-handle],.sel-edge-left,.sel-edge-right,.sel-edge-top,.sel-edge-bottom';
    scroller.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(e.button>0){tap=null;return;}
      const clip=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest(clipSelector):null;
      if(!clip||!clip.closest('#timelineInner')||(e.target.closest&&e.target.closest(excludedSelector))){tap=null;return;}
      tap={pointerId:e.pointerId,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,clientX:e.clientX,startedAt:performance.now(),moved:false,clip};
    },{capture:true,passive:true});
    scroller.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!tap||tap.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
      tap.clientX=e.clientX;
      if(Math.hypot(e.clientX-tap.startX,e.clientY-tap.startY)>=7)tap.moved=true;
    },{capture:true,passive:true});
    document.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{
      if(!tap||tap.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
      const d=tap;tap=null;
      if(d.moved||performance.now()-d.startedAt>=240||!d.clip.isConnected)return;
      const duration=typeof totalProjectDuration==='function'?totalProjectDuration():0;
      const requested=typeof getAbsoluteTimeFromPointer==='function'?getAbsoluteTimeFromPointer(e.clientX):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
      const target=Math.max(0,Math.min(Number(duration)||0,Number(requested)||0));
      try{
        const current=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        // A tap directly under the fixed playhead may select a different layer without
        // changing time. In that case there is no new preview frame to render; forcing a
        // full renderOverlayMedia pass here caused a visible pause on media-heavy projects.
        if(!Number.isFinite(current)||Math.abs(current-target)>0.000001){
          if(typeof setCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')setCurrentAbsoluteTime(target);
          if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
        }
      }catch(_e){}
    },false);
    document.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{if(tap&&tap.pointerId===e.pointerId)tap=null;},false);
  }

  function boot(){
    queuePaletteLayout();syncGlyphEffects();syncTimelineSelection();wireSlideSelection();
    ['renderFrame','renderOverlayMedia','renderOverlayLayers','renderTimelineClip','renderBackgroundTrack'].forEach(wrapRender);
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock&&!dock.dataset.evV32PaletteObserver){dock.dataset.evV32PaletteObserver='1';new MutationObserver(queuePaletteLayout).observe(dock,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});}
    const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');
    if(layer&&!layer.dataset.evV32EffectsObserver){layer.dataset.evV32EffectsObserver='1';new MutationObserver(()=>raf(syncGlyphEffects)).observe(layer,{subtree:true,childList:true});}
    const scroller=$('timelineScroll');if(!scroller){const bodyObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{wireSlideSelection();});bodyObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});}
    document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const tab=e.target?.closest?.('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab');if(!tab)return;const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(tab.dataset.tab!=='font'){restoreFontPalette($('evpaPalette')?.querySelector('.evpa-swatches'));dock?.classList.remove('evpa-font-palette-mode');}queuePaletteLayout();},true);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  /* Ensure if the editor/timeline is assembled after this layer loads, it still receives the handlers. */
  const readyObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{wireSlideSelection();queuePaletteLayout();});
  if(document.body)readyObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

    background:transparent!important;border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-font-color-tools {
    flex:0 0 35px!important;height:35px!important;min-height:35px!important;max-height:35px!important;
    display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;gap:8px!important;padding:0 2px!important;
    overflow:visible!important;background:transparent!important;border:0!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-font-color-tools > button {
    flex:0 0 35px!important;width:35px!important;height:35px!important;border-radius:50%!important;
    pointer-events:auto!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-font-color-row {
    display:flex!important;flex:0 0 35px!important;flex-flow:row nowrap!important;align-items:center!important;
    gap:8px!important;width:100%!important;height:35px!important;min-height:35px!important;max-height:35px!important;
    padding:0 2px!important;margin:0!important;overflow-x:auto!important;overflow-y:hidden!important;
    touch-action:pan-x!important;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch!important;scrollbar-width:none!important;
    background:transparent!important;border:0!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-font-color-row::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none!important;}
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-font-color-row > .evpa-swatch {
    flex:0 0 35px!important;width:35px!important;min-width:35px!important;max-width:35px!important;
    height:35px!important;min-height:35px!important;max-height:35px!important;
    border-radius:50%!important;aspect-ratio:1/1!important;pointer-events:auto!important;
  }

  /* Highlight / Stroke / Shadow strip. */
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-bottom-palette-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) .evpa-palette,
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-bottom-palette-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) #evpaPalette {
    overflow:visible!important;background:transparent!important;border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-bottom-palette-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) #evpaPalette .evpa-swatches {
    display:flex!important;flex-direction:row!important;flex-wrap:nowrap!important;align-items:center!important;
    gap:8px!important;height:48px!important;min-height:48px!important;max-height:48px!important;
    padding:0 8px!important;overflow-x:auto!important;overflow-y:visible!important;touch-action:pan-x!important;
    background:transparent!important;border:0!important;box-shadow:none!important;
  }
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock.evpa-bottom-palette-mode:not(.evpa-in-composer) #evpaPalette .evpa-swatches > button {
    flex:0 0 35px!important;width:35px!important;height:35px!important;border-radius:50%!important;aspect-ratio:1/1!important;
    pointer-events:auto!important;
  }

  /* Ranges stay interactive; JS only rejects track taps away from the thumb. */
  #ethvellePicsartTextDock input[type="range"],
  #ethvelleTextStylePanel input[type="range"],
  #ethvelleTextComposer input[type="range"],
  #evpaCustomEffectEditor input[type="range"],
  #ethvelleColorPicker input[type="range"]{
    pointer-events:auto!important;
    touch-action:none!important;
    -webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent!important;
  }
  .evpa-range-interceptor{display:none!important;pointer-events:none!important;}
  /* Remove any leftover full-screen hit layers from the broken v33. */
  .ev-v33-thumb-hit{display:none!important;pointer-events:none!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-text-section-update-v33-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const raf=fn=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{fn();}catch(_e){}});
  const clamp=(n,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n));
  const RANGE_ROOTS='body';

  /* Clear broken overlays from the previous build that blocked the whole panel. */
  document.querySelectorAll('.ev-v33-thumb-hit').forEach(el=>el.remove());

  function isToolButton(el){
    if(!el||el.nodeType!==1)return false;
    if(el.classList.contains('evpa-swatch')&&el.hasAttribute('data-color'))return false;
    return el.classList.contains('evpa-color-wheel')||el.classList.contains('evpa-transparent')||
      el.classList.contains('evpa-eye')||el.classList.contains('evpa-save-swatch')||
      el.classList.contains('evpa-swatch-action')||!el.classList.contains('evpa-swatch');
  }
  function activeTab(dock){return dock?.querySelector('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';}

  function restoreSwatchChildren(swatches){
    if(!swatches)return;
    const tools=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-utilities, :scope > .evpa-font-color-tools');
    const grid=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-grid, :scope > .evpa-font-color-row');
    if(!tools&&!grid)return;
    const nodes=[...(tools?[...tools.children]:[]),...(grid?[...grid.children]:[])];
    nodes.sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.dataset.evV33Order)||0)-(Number(b.dataset.evV33Order)||0));
    nodes.forEach(n=>{delete n.dataset.evV33Order;swatches.appendChild(n);});
    tools?.remove();grid?.remove();
  }

  function layoutColorTab(swatches){
    if(swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-utilities')&&swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-grid')){
      const u=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-utilities');
      const g=swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-color-grid');
      if(u&&g&&u.nextElementSibling!==g){swatches.insertBefore(u,g);}
      return;
    }
    const kids=[...swatches.children].filter(el=>el.tagName==='BUTTON'||el.classList.contains('evpa-swatch'));
    if(!kids.length)return;
    kids.forEach((el,i)=>el.dataset.evV33Order=String(i));
    const utilities=document.createElement('div');utilities.className='evpa-color-utilities';utilities.setAttribute('aria-label','Color tools');
    const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='evpa-color-grid';grid.setAttribute('aria-label','Color swatches');
    kids.forEach(el=>{if(el.classList.contains('evpa-swatch')&&el.hasAttribute('data-color'))grid.appendChild(el);else utilities.appendChild(el);});
    swatches.replaceChildren(utilities,grid);
  }

  function layoutFontTab(swatches){
    if(swatches.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-row'))return;
    const kids=[...swatches.children];
    if(!kids.length)return;
    kids.forEach((el,i)=>el.dataset.evV33Order=String(i));
    const tools=kids.filter(isToolButton);
    const colours=kids.filter(el=>!isToolButton(el));
    const toolsRow=document.createElement('div');toolsRow.className='evpa-font-color-tools';toolsRow.setAttribute('aria-label','Colour actions');
    const colourRow=document.createElement('div');colourRow.className='evpa-font-color-row';colourRow.setAttribute('aria-label','Text colours');
    tools.forEach(el=>toolsRow.appendChild(el));
    colours.forEach(el=>colourRow.appendChild(el));
    while(colourRow.children.length>0&&toolsRow.children.length<8){
      toolsRow.appendChild(colourRow.firstElementChild);
    }
    swatches.replaceChildren(toolsRow,colourRow);
  }

  function layoutBottomStrip(swatches){
    const buttons=[...swatches.children].filter(el=>el.tagName==='BUTTON');
    if(!buttons.length)return;
    const tools=buttons.filter(isToolButton);
    const colours=buttons.filter(el=>!isToolButton(el));
    if(!tools.length)return;
    const ordered=[...tools,...colours];
    let dirty=false;
    ordered.forEach((el,i)=>{if(swatches.children[i]!==el)dirty=true;});
    if(dirty)ordered.forEach(el=>swatches.appendChild(el));
  }

  function syncPaletteLayout(){
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');if(!dock)return;
    const tab=activeTab(dock);
    const colorMode=!dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')&&!dock.classList.contains('evpa-no-palette')&&tab==='color';
    const fontMode=!dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')&&tab==='font';
    const bottomMode=!dock.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')&&!dock.classList.contains('evpa-no-palette')&&['highlight','stroke','shadow'].includes(tab);
    dock.classList.toggle('evpa-color-grid-mode',colorMode);
    dock.classList.toggle('evpa-font-palette-mode',fontMode);
    dock.classList.toggle('evpa-bottom-palette-mode',bottomMode);
    const swatches=$('evpaPalette')?.querySelector('.evpa-swatches');
    if(!swatches)return;
    if(colorMode){layoutColorTab(swatches);}
    else if(fontMode){layoutFontTab(swatches);}
    else if(bottomMode){restoreSwatchChildren(swatches);layoutBottomStrip(swatches);}
    else {restoreSwatchChildren(swatches);}
  }
  let layoutQueued=false;
  function queueLayout(){if(layoutQueued)return;layoutQueued=true;raf(()=>{layoutQueued=false;syncPaletteLayout();});}

  /* Thumb-only ranges: no overlay layers. Reject track taps; allow thumb drags only. */
  function geometry(input){
    const r=input.getBoundingClientRect();
    let thumbW=0;
    try{thumbW=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(input,'::-webkit-slider-thumb').width)||0;}catch(_e){}
    if(!thumbW)try{thumbW=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(input,'::-moz-range-thumb').width)||0;}catch(_e){}
    const half=Math.max(7,Math.min(16,(thumbW||18)/2));
    const cs=getComputedStyle(input);
    const padLeft=parseFloat(cs.paddingLeft)||0,padRight=parseFloat(cs.paddingRight)||0;
    const trackLeft=r.left+padLeft+half;
    const trackRight=Math.max(trackLeft+1,r.right-padRight-half);
    const min=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.min))?parseFloat(input.min):0;
    const max=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.max))?parseFloat(input.max):100;
    const value=Number.isFinite(parseFloat(input.value))?parseFloat(input.value):min;
    let frac=max===min?0:clamp((value-min)/(max-min),0,1);
    const rtl=cs.direction==='rtl';
    if(rtl)frac=1-frac;
    return {r,trackLeft,trackRight,min,max,step:input.step==='any'?0:(Number(input.step)||1),thumbX:trackLeft+frac*(trackRight-trackLeft),thumbY:r.top+r.height/2,half,rtl};
  }
  function nearThumb(input,x,y,g){
    g=g||geometry(input);
    // Generous finger hit-area around the visible circular thumb, never on the rest of the rail.
    const hitX=Math.max(9,Math.min(12,g.half+2));
    const hitY=Math.max(10,Math.min(13,g.r.height/2+3));
    return Math.abs(x-g.thumbX)<=hitX && Math.abs(y-g.thumbY)<=hitY;
  }
  function dispatch(input,type){input.dispatchEvent(new Event(type,{bubbles:true}));}
  function setFromX(input,clientX,g){
    g=g||geometry(input);
    let frac=clamp((clientX-g.trackLeft)/(g.trackRight-g.trackLeft),0,1);
    if(g.rtl)frac=1-frac;
    let value=g.min+frac*(g.max-g.min);
    if(g.step>0)value=g.min+Math.round((value-g.min)/g.step)*g.step;
    value=clamp(value,g.min,g.max);
    const decimals=(String(input.step||'1').split('.')[1]||'').length;
    const next=String(Number(value.toFixed(Math.min(7,decimals||0))));
    if(input.value!==next){input.value=next;dispatch(input,'input');}
  }
  function isEditorRange(el){return !!(el&&el.type==='range'&&el.closest&&el.closest(RANGE_ROOTS));}
  let drag=null,dragRaf=0;
  function stopPointer(e){
    if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  function paintPendingDrag(){
    dragRaf=0;
    const d=drag;
    if(!d||!d.moved)return;
    setFromX(d.input,d.lastX-d.pointerOffset,d.geometry);
  }
  function flushDrag(cancelled){
    const d=drag;if(!d)return;
    if(dragRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(dragRaf);dragRaf=0;}
    drag=null;
    if(cancelled){
      if(d.input.value!==d.startValue){d.input.value=d.startValue;dispatch(d.input,'input');dispatch(d.input,'change');}
    }else if(d.moved){
      setFromX(d.input,d.lastX-d.pointerOffset,d.geometry);
      dispatch(d.input,'change');
    }
    try{d.input.releasePointerCapture(d.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
  }
  function onPointerDown(e){
    const input=e.target;
    if(!isEditorRange(input)||input.disabled||input.matches(':disabled'))return;
    if(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0)return;
    const g=geometry(input);
    if(!nearThumb(input,e.clientX,e.clientY,g)){
      stopPointer(e);return;
    }
    drag={pointerId:e.pointerId,input,geometry:g,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,lastX:e.clientX,
      pointerOffset:e.clientX-g.thumbX,startValue:String(input.value),moved:false,downEvent:e,startedInline:false};
    try{input.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    stopPointer(e);
  }
  function onPointerMove(e){
    const d=drag;if(!d||d.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    stopPointer(e);
    d.lastX=e.clientX;
    if(!d.moved&&Math.hypot(e.clientX-d.startX,e.clientY-d.startY)<2)return;
    d.moved=true;
    // Preserve the legacy Position-slider start hook, but run it only once a real drag begins.
    if(!d.startedInline){
      d.startedInline=true;
      try{if(typeof d.input.onpointerdown==='function')d.input.onpointerdown.call(d.input,d.downEvent);}catch(err){console.warn('Slider start hook:',err);}
    }
    if(!dragRaf)dragRaf=requestAnimationFrame(paintPendingDrag);
  }
  function onPointerUp(e,cancelled){
    if(!drag||drag.pointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
    stopPointer(e);
    if(Number.isFinite(e.clientX))drag.lastX=e.clientX;
    flushDrag(!!cancelled);
  }

  function boot(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ev-v33-thumb-hit').forEach(el=>el.remove());
    queueLayout();
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown',onPointerDown,{capture:true,passive:false});
    window.addEventListener('pointermove',onPointerMove,{capture:true,passive:false});
    window.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>onPointerUp(e,false),{capture:true,passive:false});
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>onPointerUp(e,true),{capture:true,passive:false});
    window.addEventListener('touchstart',function(e){
      const t=e.target;
      if(!t || t.type!=='range' || !t.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock,#ethvelleTextStylePanel,#ethvelleTextComposer,#evpaCustomEffectEditor,#ethvelleColorPicker,#textPanelBody')) return;
      const tc=e.touches && e.touches[0]; if(!tc) return;
      if(!nearThumb(t,tc.clientX,tc.clientY)){ e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); }
    },{capture:true,passive:false});
    window.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',e=>{if(drag&&drag.pointerId===e.pointerId)flushDrag(true);},{capture:true});
    // Never let a synthetic/native click change a range after the pointerdown guard.
    // Keyboard arrow-key changes still work because they do not produce pointer clicks.
    window.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      if(isEditorRange(e.target)){
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      }
    },{capture:true,passive:false});
    // Keep older interceptor refresh from re-enabling track-jump shields.
    const prevRefresh=window.__ethvelleRefreshRangeOverlays;
    window.__ethvelleRefreshRangeOverlays=function(){
      try{if(typeof prevRefresh==='function')prevRefresh();}catch(_e){}
      document.querySelectorAll('.evpa-range-interceptor,.ev-v33-thumb-hit').forEach(el=>el.remove());
      document.querySelectorAll(RANGE_ROOTS).forEach(root=>{
        root?.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]').forEach(inp=>{
          if(inp.closest('#ethvellePicsartTextDock,#ethvelleTextStylePanel,#ethvelleTextComposer,#evpaCustomEffectEditor,#ethvelleColorPicker,#textPanelBody')){
            /* v191: owned by ethvelle-v191-text-slider-js */
            inp.style.setProperty('pointer-events','none','important');
            inp.style.setProperty('touch-action','pan-y','important');
            return;
          }
          inp.style.setProperty('pointer-events','auto','important');
          inp.style.setProperty('touch-action','none','important');
          inp.style.setProperty('pointer-events','auto','important');
        });
      });
    };
    const dock=$('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
    if(dock){
      new MutationObserver(queueLayout).observe(dock,{subtree:true,childList:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});
    }
    document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      if(e.target?.closest?.('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab'))queueLayout();
    },true);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  if(document.body){
    new MutationObserver(()=>{
      document.querySelectorAll('.ev-v33-thumb-hit').forEach(el=>el.remove());
      queueLayout();
    }).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
})();
</script>

     reliable transparent swatch, clean tab state, and strict thumb-only slider input. -->

<script id="ethvelle-text-editor-ux-v34-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const raf=fn=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{fn();}catch(_e){}});
  const safeCall=(fn,...args)=>{try{return typeof fn==='function'?fn(...args):undefined;}catch(_e){return undefined;}};
  const dock=()=> $('ethvellePicsartTextDock');
  const activeTab=()=>dock()?.querySelector('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab.active')?.dataset.tab||'';
  const getTarget=()=>safeCall(window.ethvellePicsartGetTarget)||null;

  // Build the first Font row with the existing custom/transparent buttons plus clones of the
  // first six normal swatches. Clones keep the original inline click action and stay in sync.
  function syncFontPalette(){
    if(window.__ETHVELLE_V15_LOCKED_PALETTE)return;
    const d=dock();
    if(!d||d.classList.contains('evpa-in-composer')||activeTab()!=='font')return;
    const swatches=$('evpaPalette')?.querySelector('.evpa-swatches');
    const tools=swatches?.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-tools');
    const row=swatches?.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-font-color-row');
    if(!swatches||!tools||!row)return;
    tools.querySelectorAll('.evpa-font-extra-swatch').forEach(n=>n.remove());
    const wheel=tools.querySelector('.evpa-color-wheel');
    const transparent=tools.querySelector('.evpa-transparent');
    // The Font tab has no eyedropper or save-to-palette controls. Detach any leftovers
    // from older palette layouts before rebuilding its two rows.
    tools.querySelectorAll('.evpa-eye,.evpa-save-swatch').forEach(n=>n.remove());
    row.querySelectorAll('.evpa-eye,.evpa-save-swatch').forEach(n=>n.remove());
    tools.replaceChildren();
    if(wheel)tools.appendChild(wheel);
    if(transparent)tools.appendChild(transparent);
    [...row.querySelectorAll(':scope > .evpa-swatch[data-color]')].slice(0,6).forEach(original=>{
      const copy=original.cloneNode(true);
      copy.classList.add('evpa-font-extra-swatch');
      copy.removeAttribute('data-evpaFontOrder');
      copy.removeAttribute('data-evV33Order');
      copy.setAttribute('aria-label','Text colour '+(copy.dataset.color||''));
      tools.appendChild(copy);
    });
    syncTransparentState();
  }
  function removeFontClones(){
    dock()?.querySelectorAll('.evpa-font-extra-swatch').forEach(n=>n.remove());
  }
  function syncTransparentState(){
    const d=dock();if(!d)return;
    const t=getTarget();
    if(typeof window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches==='function'){window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();return;}/* v223-fix: single owner for chip markers */
    const transparent=!!t&&Number(t.textOpacity)===0&&(!activeTab()||activeTab()==='font'||activeTab()==='color');
    d.querySelectorAll('.evpa-transparent').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',transparent));
  }
  let fontQueued=false;
  function queueFont(){if(fontQueued)return;fontQueued=true;raf(()=>{fontQueued=false;syncFontPalette();syncTransparentState();});}

  // Before the legacy Font-tab restore handlers run, remove only the six temporary clones.
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(e.target?.closest?.('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab[data-tab]:not([data-tab="font"])'))removeFontClones();
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target?.closest?.('#evpaTabs .evpa-tab'))queueFont();
    if(e.target?.closest?.('#ethvellePicsartTextDock .evpa-transparent'))raf(syncTransparentState);
  },true);

  // Ensure Android's virtual keyboard resizes the composer sheet and keeps the colour controls
  // attached to the visible viewport (above the keyboard), without moving the text on the canvas.
  function syncComposerViewport(){
    const root=$('ethvelleTextComposer');
    if(!root?.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const vv=window.visualViewport;
    const top=vv?vv.offsetTop:0;
    const height=vv?vv.height:(window.innerHeight||document.documentElement.clientHeight||600);
    root.style.setProperty('top',top+'px','important');
    root.style.setProperty('height',Math.max(1,height)+'px','important');
    root.style.setProperty('bottom','auto','important');
    root.style.setProperty('left','0px','important');
    root.style.setProperty('right','0px','important');
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',syncComposerViewport,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport){
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',syncComposerViewport,{passive:true});
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',syncComposerViewport,{passive:true});
  }

  // Auto-focus synchronously while the Add Text button's user gesture is still active; this
  // opens the Android keyboard immediately. The composer viewport then keeps its dock above it.
  const priorOpen=window.openEthvelleTextComposer;
  if(typeof priorOpen==='function'&&!priorOpen.__evV34Keyboard){
    const wrappedOpen=function(){
      const args=arguments;
      const result=priorOpen.apply(this,args);
      const input=$('evtcStageText');
      if(input&&$('ethvelleTextComposer')?.classList.contains('active')){
        // Fresh text defaults to a dark fill so it can be seen on the editor's white canvas.
        // Existing non-empty text and its selected colour are never overwritten.
        const fresh=!String(input.value||'').trim()&&(!args[0]||args[0]==='__ethvelle_new_text_layer__');
        if(fresh&&typeof window.setEthvelleComposerColor==='function')safeCall(window.setEthvelleComposerColor,'#FFFFFF');
        // The Font tab is the default when the saved layer's editor dock is shown.
        safeCall(window.ethvellePicsartSetTool,'font');
        try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){safeCall(input.focus?.bind(input));}
        try{const end=input.value.length;input.setSelectionRange(end,end);}catch(_e){}
        syncComposerViewport();
        raf(syncComposerViewport);
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedOpen.__evV34Keyboard=true;
    window.openEthvelleTextComposer=wrappedOpen;
  }

  // A normal colour selection restores text opacity if the transparent swatch was active.
  const priorSetColor=window.ethvellePicsartSetColor;
  if(typeof priorSetColor==='function'&&!priorSetColor.__evV34Opacity){
    const wrappedColor=function(key,color){
      const t=getTarget();
      if(key==='textColor'&&t&&Number(t.textOpacity)===0){
        safeCall(window.ethvellePicsartSetValue,'textOpacity',100,'',true);
      }
      const result=priorSetColor.apply(this,arguments);
      raf(syncTransparentState);
      return result;
    };
    wrappedColor.__evV34Opacity=true;
    window.ethvellePicsartSetColor=wrappedColor;
  }
  const priorSetNone=window.ethvellePicsartSetNone;
  if(typeof priorSetNone==='function'&&!priorSetNone.__evV34Transparent){
    const wrappedNone=function(){const result=priorSetNone.apply(this,arguments);raf(syncTransparentState);queueFont();return result;};
    wrappedNone.__evV34Transparent=true;
    window.ethvellePicsartSetNone=wrappedNone;
  }
  const priorSetTool=window.ethvellePicsartSetTool;
  if(typeof priorSetTool==='function'&&!priorSetTool.__evV34Layout){
    const wrappedTool=function(){const result=priorSetTool.apply(this,arguments);queueFont();return result;};
    wrappedTool.__evV34Layout=true;
    window.ethvellePicsartSetTool=wrappedTool;
  }
  const priorSaveColour=window.ethvellePicsartSaveCustomColor;
  if(typeof priorSaveColour==='function'&&!priorSaveColour.__evV34Layout){
    const wrappedSaveColour=function(){const result=priorSaveColour.apply(this,arguments);queueFont();return result;};
    wrappedSaveColour.__evV34Layout=true;
    window.ethvellePicsartSaveCustomColor=wrappedSaveColour;
  }

  // Make sure the live canvas text glyph uses the model's real fill colour. This also clears a
  // stale transparent text-fill left behind by a prior gradient/texture selection, but does not
  // interfere with gradient or texture layers or with a user's intentional zero opacity.
  function enforceCanvasTextFill(){
    const layer=$('overlayMediaLayer');if(!layer)return;
    let list=[];
    try{if(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))list=overlays;else if(Array.isArray(window.overlays))list=window.overlays;}catch(_e){list=Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[];}
    list.filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text').forEach(o=>{
      const host=[...layer.children].find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId||n.dataset.ovId||'')===String(o.id));
      const node=host?.querySelector?.('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content');
      const glyph=node?.querySelector?.(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
      if(!node||!glyph)return;
      if(o.textGradient||o.textTexture)return;
      const hex=String(o.textColor||'#17171C');
      const match=hex.match(/^#([0-9a-f]{6})$/i);
      if(!match)return;
      const h=match[1],opacity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(o.textOpacity==null?100:o.textOpacity)))/100;
      const fill='rgba('+parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16)+','+parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16)+','+parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16)+','+opacity+')';
      node.style.setProperty('color',fill,'important');
      glyph.style.setProperty('color',fill,'important');
      glyph.style.setProperty('-webkit-text-fill-color',fill,'important');
      glyph.style.setProperty('background-image','none','important');
      glyph.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');
    });
  }
  ['renderFrame','renderOverlayLayers'].forEach(name=>{
    const prior=window[name];
    if(typeof prior!=='function'||prior.__evV34Fill)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const result=prior.apply(this,arguments);enforceCanvasTextFill();return result;};
    wrapped.__evV34Fill=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  });

  // If text selection opens the dock, start from Font; direct tab taps still keep their choice.
  const priorSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof priorSelect==='function'&&!priorSelect.__evV34FontDefault){
    const wrappedSelect=function(){
      const result=priorSelect.apply(this,arguments);
      const id=String(arguments[0]??window.selectedClip??'');
      let list=[];
      try{if(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))list=overlays;else if(Array.isArray(window.overlays))list=window.overlays;}catch(_e){list=Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[];}
      const selected=list.find(o=>o&&o.type==='text'&&String(o.id)===id);
      /* v231: layer selection must not initialize or rebuild the text dock. */if(selected&&window.__evTextEditorOpen===true){safeCall(window.ethvellePicsartSetTool,'font');queueFont();}
      return result;
    };
    wrappedSelect.__evV34FontDefault=true;
    window.selectClipForChrome=wrappedSelect;
  }

  function boot(){queueFont();syncComposerViewport();syncTransparentState();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
  // Avoid observing palette child mutations directly: this layout itself clones swatches,
  // so a broad body observer would repeatedly rebuild the same row.
})();
</script>
