  function setPendingOutput(target,blob,quality){
    if(pendingPreviewURL){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(pendingPreviewURL);}catch(e){}}
    pendingPreviewURL=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    pendingEnhancement={target,blob,quality:Number(quality)||1080};
    try{Object.defineProperty(target.model,'__evltPendingEnhancedSrc',{value:pendingPreviewURL,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});}catch(e){target.model.__evltPendingEnhancedSrc=pendingPreviewURL;}
    try{Object.defineProperty(target.model,'__evltPendingEnhancedQuality',{value:Number(quality)||1080,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});}catch(e){target.model.__evltPendingEnhancedQuality=Number(quality)||1080;}
    try{Object.defineProperty(target.model,'__evltEnhanceChosenQuality',{value:Number(quality)||1080,writable:true,configurable:true,enumerable:false});}catch(e){target.model.__evltEnhanceChosenQuality=Number(quality)||1080;}
    try{if(typeof window.__ethvelleV46RefreshQualityBadges==='function')window.__ethvelleV46RefreshQualityBadges();}catch(_e){}
    const apply=$('evltEnhanceApply');if(apply)apply.disabled=false;
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyVideoEnhance=async function(){
    const pending=pendingEnhancement;if(!pending){status('Tap Enhance first, then Apply.',null);return;}
    const current=resolvePendingTarget(pending);if(!current){status('The enhanced clip is no longer available in this project.',null);return;}
    const apply=$('evltEnhanceApply');if(apply){apply.disabled=true;apply.textContent='Applying…';}
    try{
      const stored=await storeEnhancedMedia(current,pending.blob,pending.quality);
      if(current.kind==='overlay'&&current.type==='video'){
        const m=current.model;
        if(m.enhancedOriginalSourceOffset==null)m.enhancedOriginalSourceOffset=Number(m.sourceOffset)||0;
        if(m.enhancedOriginalSpeed==null)m.enhancedOriginalSpeed=Number(m.speed)||1;
        m.sourceOffset=0;m.speed=1;
      }
      setRuntimeOutput(current,pending.blob,pending.quality,stored);
      try{delete current.model.__evltPendingEnhancedSrc;delete current.model.__evltPendingEnhancedQuality;}catch(e){}
      if(pendingPreviewURL){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(pendingPreviewURL);}catch(e){}pendingPreviewURL=null;}
      pendingEnhancement=null;
      status(`${qualityLabel(pending.quality)} · Applied`,100);
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(stored?'Enhancement applied to the clip':'Enhancement applied for this session; device storage was unavailable.',{duration:stored?1800:2600});
      setTimeout(syncComparePanel,50);
      try{if(typeof window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow==='function')window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow();}catch(e){}
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle Apply:',err);status(err&&err.message?err.message:'Could not apply the enhancement.',null);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(err&&err.message?err.message:'Could not apply enhancement',{duration:2400});}
    finally{if(apply){apply.disabled=!pendingEnhancement;apply.textContent='Apply';}}
  };
  /* Videos restored after a refresh (and enhanced clips, which are MediaRecorder .webm blobs) often report
     duration = Infinity/0 or have no decoded frame size yet, which used to fail with "Video has no readable
     frames". Wait for the first frame, force the browser to compute the real duration, and as a last
     resort fall back to the clip's own trim length. */
  async function evPrepareReadableVideo(v,model){
    const waitEvt=(name,ms)=>new Promise(res=>{let d=false;const fin=()=>{if(d)return;d=true;v.removeEventListener(name,fin);clearTimeout(g);res();};const g=setTimeout(fin,ms);v.addEventListener(name,fin,{once:true});});
    if(!v.videoWidth||!v.videoHeight||v.readyState<2){if(v.readyState<2)await waitEvt('loadeddata',5000);}
    if(!Number.isFinite(v.duration)||v.duration<=0){
      await new Promise(res=>{let d=false;const fin=()=>{if(d)return;d=true;v.removeEventListener('durationchange',chk);v.removeEventListener('seeked',fin);clearTimeout(g);res();};const chk=()=>{if(Number.isFinite(v.duration)&&v.duration>0)fin();};const g=setTimeout(fin,3500);v.addEventListener('durationchange',chk);v.addEventListener('seeked',fin);try{v.currentTime=1e7;}catch(_e){fin();}});
      const seekedBack=waitEvt('seeked',1500);try{v.currentTime=0;}catch(_e){}await seekedBack;
    }
    if(!Number.isFinite(v.duration)||v.duration<=0){
      const span=Math.max(0,Number(model&&model.trimEnd)-Number(model&&model.trimStart))*Math.max(.05,Number(model&&model.speed)||1),fallback=(Number(model&&model.sourceOffset)||0)+span;
      if(fallback>0){try{Object.defineProperty(v,'duration',{configurable:true,get:()=>fallback});}catch(_e){}}
    }
    if(!v.videoWidth||!v.videoHeight){await waitEvt('loadeddata',2500);}
  }
  /* v286 ENCODER FIX: Chrome (especially on Android) says H.264 is "supported" but its hardware encoder rejects
     2K/4K frames with "The given encoder configuration is not supported by the encoder." Before recording we now
     really test each size/codec on a throw-away canvas stream and use the first combination that actually starts
     encoding (H.264 hardware -> VP8 -> VP9 -> WebM default), stepping the size down 4K -> 2K -> 1080p -> 720p only
     if nothing at the requested size can encode. */
  const EV_ENHANCE_LADDER=[2160,1440,1080,720];
  const EV_OUT_FPS=30; /* v289: output is capped at 30 fps; faster sources are decimated, which halves the work for 50/60 fps clips */
  const evFastPathOn=()=>{try{if(window.EV_ENHANCE_FAST_PATH===false)return false;if(localStorage.getItem('ethvelle.enhanceFastPath')==='0')return false;}catch(_e){}return true;};
  const evEncProbeCache=new Map();
  async function evProbeEnhanceEncoder(w,h,mime,bitrate){
    let c=null,s=null,t=null,r=null,timer=0,killer=0,failed=false,gotData=false;
    try{
      if(typeof MediaRecorder==='undefined')return false;
      c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;const x=c.getContext('2d');if(!x)return false;
      x.fillStyle='#202020';x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      s=c.captureStream(0);t=s.getVideoTracks&&s.getVideoTracks()[0];
      r=mime?new MediaRecorder(s,{mimeType:mime,videoBitsPerSecond:bitrate}):new MediaRecorder(s,{videoBitsPerSecond:bitrate});
      await new Promise(resolve=>{
        let done=false;const fin=()=>{if(done)return;done=true;clearInterval(timer);clearTimeout(killer);resolve();};
        r.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size>0){gotData=true;fin();}};
        r.onerror=()=>{failed=true;fin();};
        let n=0;
        const tick=()=>{try{x.fillStyle='hsl('+((n*37)%360)+',60%,40%)';x.fillRect(0,0,w,h);x.fillStyle='#fff';x.fillRect((n*17)%Math.max(1,w-64),(n*29)%Math.max(1,h-64),64,64);if(t&&t.requestFrame)t.requestFrame();}catch(_e){}n++;};
        try{r.start(100);}catch(_e){failed=true;fin();return;}
        tick();timer=setInterval(tick,80);killer=setTimeout(fin,1100);
      });
      if(failed)return false;
      if(r.state==='inactive'&&!gotData)return false;
      return true;
    }catch(_e){return false;}
    finally{
      clearInterval(timer);clearTimeout(killer);
      try{if(r&&r.state!=='inactive')r.stop();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(s)s.getTracks().forEach(tt=>tt.stop());}catch(_e){}
      if(c){try{c.width=c.height=1;}catch(_e){}}
    }
  }
  async function evChooseEnhanceEncoder(iw,ih,requested,maxQuality,notify){
    const cap=Math.min(Number(requested)||1080,Number(maxQuality)||99999);
    let ladder=EV_ENHANCE_LADDER.filter(q=>q<=cap);if(!ladder.length)ladder=[cap];
    const families=[
      ['video/webm;codecs=h264,opus','video/webm;codecs=avc1,opus'],
      ['video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8'],
      ['video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp9'],
      ['video/webm']
    ];
    const mimes=[];
    families.forEach(f=>{const m=f.find(v=>{try{return !MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported||MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(v);}catch(_e){return false;}});if(m)mimes.push(m);});
    if(!mimes.length)mimes.push('');
    for(const q of ladder){
      const size=outputSize(iw,ih,q);
      for(const mime of mimes){
        const isH264=/h264|avc1/i.test(mime);
        const bitrate=isH264?(q>=2160?14000000:q>=1440?9000000:6000000):(q>=2160?18000000:q>=1440?12000000:8000000);
        const key=size.w+'x'+size.h+'|'+mime;
        if(typeof notify==='function')notify(q,mime);
        let ok=evEncProbeCache.get(key);
        if(ok===undefined){ok=await evProbeEnhanceEncoder(size.w,size.h,mime,bitrate);evEncProbeCache.set(key,ok);}
        if(ok)return {quality:q,mime,bitrate,size};
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  function evNextLowerEnhanceQuality(q){const lower=EV_ENHANCE_LADDER.filter(v=>v<q);return lower.length?lower[0]:0;}
  function evQLabel(q){return q>=2160?'4K':q+'p';}
  /* v294 DEVICE COMFORT TEST. Runs once, the first time the Enhance panel opens (the panel shows a loading state meanwhile).
     It checks, per output size, whether this phone can really handle it: GPU texture limit, RAM, canvas throughput and a real
     MediaRecorder encoder probe. Result: window.__evEnhanceCaps = {state:'idle|running|done|error', ok:{1080,1440,2160}}.
       - 1080p not comfortable  -> Enhance still SAYS 1080p, but quietly encodes at 720p (no toast, no status change).
       - 1440p / 4K not comfortable -> an error is shown BEFORE anything starts, nothing is encoded. */
  window.__evEnhanceCaps={state:'idle',ok:null};
  let evCapsPromise=null;
  function evEncMimeList(){
    const families=[['video/webm;codecs=h264,opus','video/webm;codecs=avc1,opus'],['video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8'],['video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp9'],['video/webm']];
    const mimes=[];families.forEach(f=>{const m=f.find(v=>{try{return !MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported||MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(v);}catch(_e){return false;}});if(m)mimes.push(m);});
    if(!mimes.length)mimes.push('');return mimes;
  }
  async function evQualityComfortable(q){
    const size=outputSize(1920,1080,q);
    try{
      const gc=document.createElement('canvas');const gl=gc.getContext('webgl2')||gc.getContext('webgl');
      if(gl){const mt=Number(gl.getParameter(gl.MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE))||0,rb=Number(gl.getParameter(gl.MAX_RENDERBUFFER_SIZE))||0;
        try{const ext=gl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context');if(ext)ext.loseContext();}catch(_e){}
        if((mt&&mt<size.w)||(rb&&rb<size.w))return false;}
    }catch(_e){}
    const mem=Number(navigator.deviceMemory)||0;
    if(mem&&((q>=2160&&mem<4)||(q>=1440&&mem<3)||(q>=1080&&mem<1.5)))return false;
    try{
      const a=document.createElement('canvas'),b=document.createElement('canvas');a.width=b.width=size.w;a.height=b.height=size.h;
      const xa=a.getContext('2d'),xb=b.getContext('2d');if(!xa||!xb)return false;
      const t0=performance.now();
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++){xa.fillStyle='hsl('+(i*70)+',60%,45%)';xa.fillRect(0,0,size.w,size.h);xb.drawImage(a,0,0);}
      xb.getImageData(0,0,1,1);
      const per=(performance.now()-t0)/4;
      a.width=a.height=b.width=b.height=1;
      if(per>(q>=2160?450:q>=1440?350:300))return false;
    }catch(_e){return false;}
    for(const mime of evEncMimeList()){
      const isH264=/h264|avc1/i.test(mime);
      const bitrate=isH264?(q>=2160?14000000:q>=1440?9000000:6000000):(q>=2160?18000000:q>=1440?12000000:8000000);
      const key=size.w+'x'+size.h+'|'+mime;
      let ok=evEncProbeCache.get(key);
      if(ok===undefined){ok=await evProbeEnhanceEncoder(size.w,size.h,mime,bitrate);evEncProbeCache.set(key,ok);}
      if(ok)return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  window.ethvelleEnsureEnhanceCaps=function(){
    if(evCapsPromise)return evCapsPromise;
    const caps=window.__evEnhanceCaps;caps.state='running';
    const t0=performance.now();
    evCapsPromise=(async()=>{
      const ok={1080:false,1440:false,2160:false};
      try{
        let prev=true;
        for(const q of [1080,1440,2160]){ok[q]=prev?await evQualityComfortable(q):false;prev=ok[q];}
        caps.ok=ok;caps.state='done';
      }catch(_e){caps.ok=null;caps.state='error';}
      const wait=500-(performance.now()-t0);if(wait>0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,wait));
      return caps;
    })();
    return evCapsPromise;
  };
  function evEnhanceUnsupported(rq){
    const msg=evQLabel(rq)+' enhancement isn\'t supported on this device. Choose 1080p instead.';
    status(msg,null);
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(msg,{duration:3400,icon:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#FF5C6C" stroke-width="2.3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M12 7.5v5.5M12 16.5v.01"/></svg>'});
  }
  window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance=async function evltStartEnhanceCore(opts){
    opts=(opts&&typeof opts==='object'&&typeof opts.maxQuality==='number')?opts:{};
    const target=liveTarget();if(!target){status('');return;}if(window.__ethvelleV5EnhanceBusy)return;
    /* v294: make sure the device comfort test has finished, and refuse >1080p BEFORE doing any work if this device can't do it. */
    if(target.type==='video'&&typeof opts.maxQuality!=='number'){
      try{
        const rq0=Number($('evltEnhanceQuality')?.value)||1080;
        const caps0=await window.ethvelleEnsureEnhanceCaps();
        if(rq0>1080&&caps0&&caps0.ok&&caps0.ok[rq0]===false){evEnhanceUnsupported(rq0);return;}
      }catch(_e){}
      if(window.__ethvelleV5EnhanceBusy)return;
    }
    window.__ethvelleV5EnhanceBusy=true;
    const btn=$('evltEnhanceStart'),amount=clamp($('evltEnhanceAmount')?.value??target.model.enhanceAmount??0,0,100);
    let quality=Number($('evltEnhanceQuality')?.value)||1080,label=qualityLabel(quality),chosenEnc=null,retryMax=0;
    const requestedQuality=quality;
    try{if(typeof window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime==='function')window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime();else if(typeof window.pausePlayback==='function')window.pausePlayback();}catch(_e){}
    const mediaAudioSnapshot=[];
    try{document.querySelectorAll('audio,video').forEach(media=>{mediaAudioSnapshot.push({media,muted:!!media.muted,volume:Number(media.volume)});try{media.pause();media.muted=true;media.volume=0;}catch(_e){}});}catch(_e){}
    if(btn){btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Enhancing…';}status('',0);
    let sourceVideo=null,outStream=null,sourceStream=null,recorder=null,raf=0,rvfc=0,watchdog=0,stopResolve=null,stopped=false,pipeline=null,audioContext=null,audioSource=null,audioDestination=null,silentGain=null,canvas=null,ctx=null,canvasTrack=null;
    let stopMinTimer=0,stopFlushTimer=0,stopScheduled=false,recordingStartedAt=0,endPoll=0;
    let directCapture=false,usedLite=false,liteAvailable=false,retryOpts={},evStatusAt=0,evLastDrawMedia=-1,evLastWall=0,evBaseAt=0,evBaseFrames=0,evBasePresented=-1,evBaseWall=0,evExpectedFps=0;
    let evFramesDrawn=0,evFirstDrawAt=0,evPresentedFirst=-1,evPresentedLast=-1,evThroughputChecked=false,evSlowAbort=false,evMeasuredFps=0,evTargetFps=24,evFpsKnown=false;
    try{
      if(target.type==='image'){
        const img=new Image();img.decoding='async';if(/^https?:/i.test(target.src))img.crossOrigin='anonymous';
        await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{img.onload=resolve;img.onerror=()=>reject(new Error('Image cannot be processed in this browser.'));img.src=target.src;if(img.complete&&img.naturalWidth)resolve();});
        const size=outputSize(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height,quality);
        pipeline=createEnhancePipeline(img.naturalWidth||img.width,img.naturalHeight||img.height,size.w,size.h);
        pipeline.render(img,filterFor(target.model,amount),amount);
        const finalCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');finalCanvas.width=size.w;finalCanvas.height=size.h;const finalCtx=finalCanvas.getContext('2d');
        if(!finalCtx)throw new Error('Image output canvas is unavailable.');finalCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;finalCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';finalCtx.drawImage(pipeline.canvas,0,0,size.w,size.h);
        const blob=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>finalCanvas.toBlob(b=>b?resolve(b):reject(new Error('Image export failed.')),'image/png'));
        pipeline.dispose();pipeline=null;if(!blob||!blob.size)throw new Error('Image export failed.');
        setPendingOutput(target,blob,quality);status(`${label} · ${size.w} × ${size.h} · Ready to apply`,100);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Enhancement ready  -  tap Apply to use it',{duration:1800});setTimeout(syncComparePanel,50);return;
      }
      if(target.type!=='video')throw new Error('Choose an image or video clip.');
      if(typeof MediaRecorder==='undefined'||!HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.captureStream)throw new Error('Video enhancement is not supported by this browser.');
      sourceVideo=document.createElement('video');sourceVideo.preload='auto';sourceVideo.playsInline=true;sourceVideo.crossOrigin=/^https?:/i.test(target.src)?'anonymous':'';sourceVideo.muted=false;sourceVideo.volume=1;sourceVideo.setAttribute('playsinline','');sourceVideo.src=target.src;
      await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let complete=false;const finish=e=>{if(complete)return;complete=true;sourceVideo.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata',loaded);sourceVideo.removeEventListener('error',failed);e?reject(e):resolve();};const loaded=()=>finish();const failed=()=>finish(new Error('Video could not be read. Local files or CORS-enabled media are required.'));sourceVideo.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',loaded,{once:true});sourceVideo.addEventListener('error',failed,{once:true});sourceVideo.load();if(sourceVideo.readyState>=1)finish();});
      await evPrepareReadableVideo(sourceVideo,target.model);
      if(!sourceVideo.videoWidth||!sourceVideo.videoHeight||!Number.isFinite(sourceVideo.duration)||sourceVideo.duration<=0)throw new Error('Video has no readable frames.');
      const sourceStart=clamp(Number(target.model.sourceOffset)||0,0,Math.max(0,sourceVideo.duration-.06)),clipSpeed=clamp(Number(target.model.speed)||1,.05,4);
      const timelineSpan=Math.max(MIN_CLIP_SEC,Number(target.model.trimEnd)-Number(target.model.trimStart)||sourceVideo.duration),sourceSpan=Math.min(Math.max(.06,sourceVideo.duration-sourceStart),timelineSpan*clipSpeed),duration=Math.max(.06,sourceSpan/clipSpeed);
      if(sourceStart>.025){await new Promise(resolve=>{let settled=false,guard=0;const done=()=>{if(settled)return;settled=true;sourceVideo.removeEventListener('seeked',done);clearTimeout(guard);resolve();};sourceVideo.addEventListener('seeked',done,{once:true});guard=setTimeout(done,2600);try{sourceVideo.currentTime=sourceStart;}catch(_e){done();}});}
      sourceVideo.playbackRate=clipSpeed;
      try{const cap=sourceVideo.captureStream||sourceVideo.mozCaptureStream;if(typeof cap==='function')sourceStream=cap.call(sourceVideo);}catch(_e){}
      let sourceFps=30;try{const vt=sourceStream&&sourceStream.getVideoTracks&&sourceStream.getVideoTracks()[0],settings=vt&&vt.getSettings?vt.getSettings():null;const detected=Number(settings&&settings.frameRate);if(Number.isFinite(detected)&&detected>=1&&detected<=120){sourceFps=detected;evFpsKnown=true;}}catch(_e){}
      status('Checking encoder…',0);
      const capsNow=window.__evEnhanceCaps;
      /* v294: 1080p requested but this device is not comfortable at 1080p -> encode at 720p quietly (the UI keeps saying 1080p). */
      const silent720=requestedQuality<=1080&&!!(capsNow&&capsNow.state==='done'&&capsNow.ok&&capsNow.ok[1080]===false);
      chosenEnc=await evChooseEnhanceEncoder(sourceVideo.videoWidth,sourceVideo.videoHeight,quality,Math.min(Number(opts.maxQuality)||99999,silent720?720:99999),()=>status('Checking '+evQLabel(requestedQuality)+' encoder…',0));
      if(!chosenEnc)throw new Error('This phone\'s browser cannot encode video here. Close other apps/tabs and try again.');
      if(chosenEnc.quality<requestedQuality){
        if(requestedQuality<=1080){quality=chosenEnc.quality;/* v294: silent 720p behind a 1080p label, no toast */}
        else{const e=new Error(evQLabel(requestedQuality)+' enhancement isn\'t supported on this device. Choose 1080p instead.');e.evUnsupported=true;throw e;}
      }
      const size=chosenEnc.size;
      const shownSize=outputSize(sourceVideo.videoWidth,sourceVideo.videoHeight,requestedQuality);
      /* v289: above QHD (4K) start with the light upscaler; it only differs from the full one when upscaling. */
      const wantLite=(typeof opts.lite==='boolean')?opts.lite:(size.w*size.h>3700000);
      liteAvailable=size.w>=sourceVideo.videoWidth&&size.h>=sourceVideo.videoHeight;
      pipeline=createEnhancePipeline(sourceVideo.videoWidth,sourceVideo.videoHeight,size.w,size.h,{lite:wantLite,direct:evFastPathOn()});
      usedLite=!!pipeline.lite;
      /* v289: record straight from the WebGL canvas (no second full-size 2D canvas copy per frame). */
      directCapture=(pipeline.renderer==='WebGL')&&!opts.noDirect&&evFastPathOn();
      if(directCapture){canvas=pipeline.canvas;ctx=null;}
      else{canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=size.w;canvas.height=size.h;ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false,desynchronized:true});if(!ctx)throw new Error('Video output canvas is unavailable.');ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';}
      let manualFrames=false;try{outStream=canvas.captureStream(0);canvasTrack=outStream.getVideoTracks&&outStream.getVideoTracks()[0];manualFrames=!!(canvasTrack&&typeof canvasTrack.requestFrame==='function');if(!manualFrames){outStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());outStream=canvas.captureStream(sourceFps);canvasTrack=outStream.getVideoTracks&&outStream.getVideoTracks()[0];}}catch(_e){outStream=canvas.captureStream(sourceFps);canvasTrack=outStream.getVideoTracks&&outStream.getVideoTracks()[0];}
      // Route the decoded source audio into the output stream without playing it through phone speakers.
      let audioAdded=false;
      try{
        const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;
        if(AC&&sourceVideo){audioContext=new AC();await audioContext.resume();audioSource=audioContext.createMediaElementSource(sourceVideo);audioDestination=audioContext.createMediaStreamDestination();audioSource.connect(audioDestination);silentGain=audioContext.createGain();silentGain.gain.value=0;audioSource.connect(silentGain);silentGain.connect(audioContext.destination);const tracks=audioDestination.stream.getAudioTracks();tracks.forEach(t=>outStream.addTrack(t));audioAdded=tracks.length>0;}
      }catch(audioErr){console.warn('Ethvelle: Web Audio routing unavailable; using the captured source audio track when present.',audioErr);}
      if(!audioAdded&&sourceStream){try{sourceStream.getAudioTracks().forEach(t=>outStream.addTrack(t));}catch(_e){}}
      const mimeCandidates=['video/webm;codecs=h264,opus','video/webm;codecs=avc1,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp9,opus','video/webm;codecs=vp8','video/webm;codecs=vp9','video/webm'];
      const isH264Mime=candidate=>candidate==='video/webm;codecs=h264,opus'||candidate==='video/webm;codecs=avc1,opus';
      const supportedMimeCandidates=mimeCandidates.filter(candidate=>{try{return isH264Mime(candidate)?!!(MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported&&MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(candidate)):(!MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported||MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(candidate));}catch(_e){return false;}});
      const mime=(chosenEnc&&chosenEnc.mime)||supportedMimeCandidates[0]||'';
      let recorderError=null;for(const candidate of [mime,...supportedMimeCandidates.filter(x=>x!==mime),'']){if(recorder||candidate===null)break;try{recorder=candidate?new MediaRecorder(outStream,{mimeType:candidate,videoBitsPerSecond:(chosenEnc&&candidate===chosenEnc.mime)?chosenEnc.bitrate:isH264Mime(candidate)?(quality>=2160?14000000:quality>=1440?9000000:6000000):(quality>=2160?18000000:quality>=1440?12000000:8000000)}):new MediaRecorder(outStream);}catch(err){recorderError=err;recorder=null;}}
      if(!recorder)throw new Error((recorderError&&recorderError.message)||'This browser could not start a compatible video encoder. Try 1080p or reopen the source clip.');
      const chunks=[];const stopPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{stopResolve=resolve;recorder.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size)chunks.push(e.data);};recorder.onerror=e=>reject(e.error||new Error('Video encoding failed.'));recorder.onstop=resolve;});
      recordingStartedAt=Date.now();try{recorder.start(500);}catch(err){throw new Error('The browser video recorder could not start: '+(err&&err.message||'unknown error'));}
      evTargetFps=Math.max(4,evFpsKnown?Math.min(30,sourceFps*clipSpeed):Math.min(24,30*clipSpeed));
      const drawFrame=()=>{if(sourceVideo.readyState<2||!sourceVideo.videoWidth)return false;pipeline.render(sourceVideo,filterFor(target.model,amount),amount);if(!directCapture){ctx.clearRect(0,0,size.w,size.h);ctx.drawImage(pipeline.canvas,0,0,size.w,size.h);}try{if(manualFrames&&canvasTrack&&canvasTrack.requestFrame)canvasTrack.requestFrame();}catch(_e){}evFramesDrawn++;if(!evFirstDrawAt)evFirstDrawAt=performance.now();return true;};
      const stopOnce=()=>{if(stopped||stopScheduled)return;const elapsed=Date.now()-recordingStartedAt;if(elapsed<750){stopScheduled=true;stopMinTimer=setTimeout(()=>{stopScheduled=false;stopOnce();},750-elapsed);return;}stopped=true;if(endPoll){clearInterval(endPoll);endPoll=0;}if(raf)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);if(rvfc&&sourceVideo&&sourceVideo.cancelVideoFrameCallback){try{sourceVideo.cancelVideoFrameCallback(rvfc);}catch(_e){}}if(watchdog)clearTimeout(watchdog);try{if(recorder&&recorder.state!=='inactive'){try{if(typeof recorder.requestData==='function')recorder.requestData();}catch(_e){}stopFlushTimer=setTimeout(()=>{try{if(recorder&&recorder.state!=='inactive')recorder.stop();else if(stopResolve)stopResolve();}catch(_e){if(stopResolve)stopResolve();}},160);}else if(stopResolve)stopResolve();}catch(_e){if(stopResolve)stopResolve();}};
      watchdog=setTimeout(()=>stopOnce(),Math.max(15000,Math.ceil(duration*1000)+10000));
      /* v288: the baked clip can only be as smooth as the number of frames this phone really processed in real time.
         If the GPU/encoder cannot sustain it at this resolution, stop early and redo it one quality step lower. */
      const evCheckThroughput=()=>{
        if(evThroughputChecked||stopped||!evFirstDrawAt)return;
        const nowT=performance.now();
        /* v289: skip the first ~1 s (decoder start-up, shader warm-up, encoder spin-up) and judge the device on a clean 2.6 s window. */
        if(!evBaseAt){if(nowT-evFirstDrawAt<1000)return;evBaseAt=nowT;evBaseFrames=evFramesDrawn;evBasePresented=evPresentedLast;evBaseWall=evLastWall;return;}
        const secs=(nowT-evBaseAt)/1000,drawn=evFramesDrawn-evBaseFrames;
        if(secs<2.6||drawn<8)return;
        evThroughputChecked=true;evMeasuredFps=drawn/secs;
        let expected=evTargetFps;
        if(evBasePresented>=0&&evLastWall>evBaseWall+400&&evPresentedLast>evBasePresented+5){
          /* what the video element really presented per second (so a decoder-limited clip is not blamed on our pipeline), capped at the 30 fps output rate */
          expected=Math.min(EV_OUT_FPS,(evPresentedLast-evBasePresented)/((evLastWall-evBaseWall)/1000));
        }
        evExpectedFps=expected;
        const slow=evMeasuredFps<expected*0.65;
        if(slow&&(evNextLowerEnhanceQuality(quality)||(!usedLite&&liteAvailable&&opts.lite!==true))){evSlowAbort=true;stopOnce();}
      };
      const reportAndContinue=()=>{evCheckThroughput();if(evSlowAbort)return false;const pct=clamp((sourceVideo.currentTime-sourceStart)/Math.max(.06,sourceSpan)*100,0,99);const tStat=performance.now();if(tStat-evStatusAt>=250){evStatusAt=tStat;status(`${Math.floor(pct)}%`,pct);}if(sourceVideo.ended||sourceVideo.currentTime>=sourceStart+sourceSpan-.025){stopOnce();return false;}return true;};
      const onVideoFrame=(_now,meta)=>{if(stopped)return;if(meta&&typeof meta.presentedFrames==='number'){if(evPresentedFirst<0)evPresentedFirst=meta.presentedFrames;evPresentedLast=meta.presentedFrames;evLastWall=_now;}
        /* v289: 50/60 fps sources are decimated to the 30 fps output rate, so the GPU and encoder only do half the frames. */
        let due=true;const hasMT=!!(meta&&typeof meta.mediaTime==='number');
        if(hasMT&&evLastDrawMedia>=0){const gap=(meta.mediaTime-evLastDrawMedia)/clipSpeed;if(gap>=0)due=gap>=(1/EV_OUT_FPS)-0.0045;}
        if(due){try{if(drawFrame()&&hasMT)evLastDrawMedia=meta.mediaTime;}catch(e){status('Video frame processing failed: '+(e&&e.message||''),null);stopOnce();return;}}
        if(!reportAndContinue())return;if(typeof sourceVideo.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function')rvfc=sourceVideo.requestVideoFrameCallback(onVideoFrame);};
      try{drawFrame();}catch(_e){}
      await sourceVideo.play();
      evFramesDrawn=0;evFirstDrawAt=0;evPresentedFirst=-1;evPresentedLast=-1;evLastDrawMedia=-1;evBaseAt=0;evLastWall=0;
      endPoll=setInterval(()=>{if(stopped){clearInterval(endPoll);endPoll=0;return;}try{if(sourceVideo&&(sourceVideo.ended||sourceVideo.currentTime>=sourceStart+sourceSpan-.025))stopOnce();}catch(_e){}},120);
      if(typeof sourceVideo.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function')rvfc=sourceVideo.requestVideoFrameCallback(onVideoFrame);
      else{let lastDraw=0;const draw=now=>{if(stopped)return;if(sourceVideo.readyState>=2&&now-lastDraw>=1000/sourceFps){lastDraw=now;try{drawFrame();}catch(e){status('Video frame processing failed: '+(e&&e.message||''),null);stopOnce();return;}if(!reportAndContinue())return;}raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);};raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);}
      await stopPromise;
      if(evSlowAbort){const slowErr=new Error('This phone could not process '+evQLabel(quality)+' in real time ('+Math.round(evMeasuredFps)+' fps).');slowErr.evSlow=true;slowErr.evFps=evMeasuredFps;throw slowErr;}
      const usableChunks=chunks.filter(c=>c&&c.size>0);if(!usableChunks.length)throw new Error('The browser recorder started but produced no video frames. Try 1080p, then reopen the clip and try again.');
      const blob=new Blob(usableChunks,{type:recorder.mimeType||mime||'video/webm'});if(!blob.size)throw new Error('The video output is empty.');
      let finalBlob=blob;try{if(typeof window.__evltMakeRecordedMediaSeekable==='function')finalBlob=(await window.__evltMakeRecordedMediaSeekable(blob))||blob;}catch(_e){finalBlob=blob;}
      try{const secsAll=evFirstDrawAt?(performance.now()-evFirstDrawAt)/1000:0;if(requestedQuality>1080&&secsAll>2.2&&evFramesDrawn/secsAll<(evExpectedFps||evTargetFps)*0.65&&typeof showToast==='function')showToast('This phone processed only '+Math.round(evFramesDrawn/secsAll)+' fps, so the enhanced clip may look choppy. A lower quality will be smoother.',{duration:4200});}catch(_e){}
      setPendingOutput(target,finalBlob,requestedQuality);status(`${label} · ${shownSize.w} × ${shownSize.h} · Ready to apply`,100);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Enhancement ready  -  tap Apply to use it',{duration:1800});setTimeout(syncComparePanel,50);
    }catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle V5 enhancement:',err);
      const msg=String(err&&(err.message||err.name)||'');
      const lowerQ=evNextLowerEnhanceQuality(quality);
      const evSlowErr=!!(err&&err.evSlow);
      if(target.type==='video'&&evSlowErr&&!usedLite&&liteAvailable&&opts.lite!==true&&pipeline&&pipeline.renderer==='WebGL'){
        /* v289: before giving up resolution, retry the SAME resolution with the lighter upscaler. */
        retryMax=quality;retryOpts={lite:true,noDirect:opts.noDirect};
        const why='Keeping '+evQLabel(quality)+'  -  switching to lighter processing';
        status(why+'…',0);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(why,{duration:2400});
      }else if(target.type==='video'&&lowerQ&&requestedQuality<=1080&&(evSlowErr||/encoder|encoding|configuration|not supported|no video frames|recorder/i.test(msg))){
        retryMax=lowerQ;retryOpts={noDirect:opts.noDirect};
        try{evEncProbeCache.forEach((v,k)=>{if(k.startsWith(chosenEnc&&chosenEnc.size?chosenEnc.size.w+'x'+chosenEnc.size.h+'|':'\u0000'))evEncProbeCache.delete(k);});}catch(_e){}
        /* v294: 1080p -> 720p happens silently; the user only ever sees "1080p". */
        status('Enhancing…',0);
      }else{
        /* v294: above 1080p there is no fallback - show a clear error instead. */
        const hardMsg=(target.type==='video'&&requestedQuality>1080&&(evSlowErr||(err&&err.evUnsupported)||/encoder|encoding|configuration|not supported|no video frames|recorder/i.test(msg)))?(evQLabel(requestedQuality)+' enhancement isn\'t supported on this device. Choose 1080p instead.'):'';
        const shownMsg=hardMsg||(err&&err.message?err.message:'Enhancement failed.');
        status(shownMsg,null);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(shownMsg,{duration:hardMsg?3400:2200});
      }
    }
    finally{
      if(raf)cancelAnimationFrame(raf);if(rvfc&&sourceVideo&&sourceVideo.cancelVideoFrameCallback){try{sourceVideo.cancelVideoFrameCallback(rvfc);}catch(_e){}}if(watchdog)clearTimeout(watchdog);if(endPoll){clearInterval(endPoll);endPoll=0;}if(stopMinTimer)clearTimeout(stopMinTimer);if(stopFlushTimer)clearTimeout(stopFlushTimer);
      try{if(recorder&&recorder.state!=='inactive')recorder.stop();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(sourceVideo){sourceVideo.pause();sourceVideo.removeAttribute('src');sourceVideo.load();}}catch(_e){}
      try{if(sourceStream)sourceStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());}catch(_e){}try{if(outStream)outStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());}catch(_e){}
      try{if(audioContext&&audioContext.state!=='closed')await audioContext.close();}catch(_e){}try{if(pipeline)pipeline.dispose();}catch(_e){}
      mediaAudioSnapshot.forEach(state=>{try{if(state.media&&state.media.isConnected){state.media.muted=state.muted;state.media.volume=Number.isFinite(state.volume)?state.volume:1;if(state.media.tagName==='VIDEO'){state.media.autoplay=false;state.media.removeAttribute('autoplay');state.media.pause();}}}catch(_e){}});
      window.__ethvelleV5EnhanceBusy=false;if(btn){btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='Enhance';}const apply=$('evltEnhanceApply');if(apply)apply.disabled=!pendingEnhancement;
    }
    if(retryMax)return evltStartEnhanceCore(Object.assign({maxQuality:retryMax},retryOpts));
  };
  // Toolbar visibility is deliberately not written from this V5 enhancement patch.
  // V80 is the single owner of bottomToolsDefault / bottomToolsClip / bottomToolsKeyframe
  // display state. Previously this patch wrote non-important inline display values and
  // observed those same writes while V80 re-applied important values, creating a feedback loop.
  // Opening Enhance renders the old base preview first; restore the proper right-side AFTER layer.
  const panel=$('toolPanel');if(panel)new MutationObserver(()=>{if($('evltCompare'))syncComparePanel();}).observe(panel,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<div id="ethvelleStoragePermission" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="evspTitle">
  <div class="evsp-sheet">
    <div class="evsp-mark" aria-hidden="true">▣</div>
    <h2 id="evspTitle">Allow device storage</h2>
    <p>Ethvelle needs local browser storage to keep your projects on this device, including timeline edits, uploaded clips, images, and project settings. When supported, the media picker can link directly to a selected device file instead of copying a long video.</p>
    <div class="evsp-info"><span aria-hidden="true">ⓘ</span><span>This approval enables Ethvelle's browser storage; it is not unrestricted Android storage permission. Browser storage can still be cleared, and direct file access depends on browser support and permission, so export important work as a backup.</span></div>
    <div class="evsp-actions"><button class="evsp-cancel" id="evspCancel" type="button">Not now</button><button class="evsp-allow" id="evspAllow" type="button">Allow &amp; continue</button></div>
  </div>

<script id="ethvelle-device-storage-enhance-fix-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const CONSENT_KEY='ethvelle.deviceStorageConsent.v1';
  const DB_NAME='ethvelle-device-project-storage-v1';
  const SOURCE_DB_NAME='ethvelle-source-media-v1';
  let dbPromise=null,sourceDbPromise=null,saveTimer=0,lastStorageErrorAt=0,pendingCreate=false;
  let canvasComparePercent=50,canvasCompareDrag=null;
  function openSourceMediaDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.reject(new Error('This browser does not support device media storage.'));
    if(!sourceDbPromise){
      sourceDbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const req=indexedDB.open(SOURCE_DB_NAME,1);
        req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('assets'))db.createObjectStore('assets');};
        req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not open device media storage.'));
        req.onblocked=()=>reject(new Error('Device media storage is busy in another tab.'));
      }).catch(err=>{sourceDbPromise=null;throw err;});
    }
    return sourceDbPromise;
  }
  async function putSourceMedia(key,blob,meta={}){
    const db=await openSourceMediaDb();
    await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('assets','readwrite');tx.objectStore('assets').put({blob,name:meta.name||'Video',type:meta.type||blob.type||'video/mp4',size:blob.size||0,lastModified:meta.lastModified||0,savedAt:Date.now()},key);tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Could not save the selected media file.'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Media save was interrupted.'));});
    return key;
  }
  async function getSourceMedia(key){const db=await openSourceMediaDb();return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('assets','readonly'),req=tx.objectStore('assets').get(key);req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result||null);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not read the saved media file.'));});}
  async function listSourceMediaRecords(){
    const db=await openSourceMediaDb();
    return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      const tx=db.transaction('assets','readonly'),req=tx.objectStore('assets').openCursor(),rows=[];
      req.onsuccess=()=>{const cur=req.result;if(!cur){resolve(rows);return;}rows.push({key:String(cur.key),...(cur.value||{})});cur.continue();};
      req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not list device media.'));
    });
  }
  function mediaFamilyMatches(model,record){
    const family=String(model&&model.type||'').toLowerCase();
    const mime=String(record&&record.type||'').toLowerCase();
    const name=String(record&&record.name||'').toLowerCase();
    if(family==='video')return mime.startsWith('video/')||/\.(mp4|m4v|f4v|mov|webm|mkv|avi|3gp|3gpp|3g2|mpeg|mpg|mpe|m2v|ogv|ts|mts|m2ts|wmv|flv)$/i.test(name);
    if(family==='audio')return mime.startsWith('audio/')||/\.(mp3|m4a|aac|wav|ogg|opus|flac|aiff?)$/i.test(name);
    if(family==='image')return mime.startsWith('image/')||/\.(png|apng|jpe?g|jfif|webp|gif|avif|bmp|dib|heic|heif|tif|tiff|svg|ico|jxl)$/i.test(name);
    return true;
  }
  function repairLegacyOverlayMediaRefs(root,records){
    let changed=false;
    const list=Array.isArray(root)?root:[root];
    for(const project of list){
      const state=project&&project.savedState;if(!state)continue;
      const updatedAt=Number(project.updatedAt)||0;
      const mediaModels=[...(Array.isArray(state.overlays)?state.overlays:[]),...(Array.isArray(state.backgrounds)?state.backgrounds:[])];
      for(const model of mediaModels){
        if(!model||typeof model.src!=='string')continue;
        const wrapped=/^url\(\s*blob:/i.test(model.src);
        const isBlob=model.src.startsWith('blob:')||wrapped;
        if(!isBlob)continue;
        let key=model.deviceMediaKey?String(model.deviceMediaKey):'';
        let record=key?records.find(r=>String(r.key)===key):null;
        if(!record){
          const names=[model.deviceMediaName,model.name].filter(Boolean).map(v=>String(v));
          let candidates=records.filter(r=>names.includes(String(r.name||''))&&mediaFamilyMatches(model,r));
          // If duplicate filenames exist, prefer the saved file closest to this project's last
          // edit time. A small timestamp window prevents an old, unrelated same-name asset from
          // being attached to the wrong clip when a more precise metadata match is unavailable.
          if(updatedAt){
            const near=candidates.filter(r=>!Number(r.savedAt)||Math.abs(Number(r.savedAt)-updatedAt)<=14*24*60*60*1000);
            if(near.length)candidates=near;
            candidates.sort((a,b)=>Math.abs((Number(a.savedAt)||0)-updatedAt)-Math.abs((Number(b.savedAt)||0)-updatedAt));
          }else candidates.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.savedAt)||0)-(Number(a.savedAt)||0));
          record=candidates[0]||null;
          if(record){key=String(record.key);model.deviceMediaKey=key;model.deviceMediaName=record.name||model.name;model.deviceMediaSize=Number(record.size)||undefined;model.deviceMediaLastModified=Number(record.lastModified)||undefined;}
        }
        if(key){
          model.src=wrapped?'url(ethvelle-device-media://'+key+')':'ethvelle-device-media://'+key;
          changed=true;
        }
      }
    }
    return changed;
  }
  function projectSnapshotWithMediaRefs(){
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(projects,function(key,value){
      if(key==='src'&&typeof value==='string'&&this&&this.deviceMediaKey){
        if(value.startsWith('blob:'))return 'ethvelle-device-media://'+this.deviceMediaKey;
        const m=value.match(/^url\((blob:[^)]+)\)$/i);if(m)return 'url(ethvelle-device-media://'+this.deviceMediaKey+')';
      }
      return value;
    }));
  }
  async function rehydrateDeviceMediaRefs(root){
    const tasks=[],seen=new Set();
    const walk=(v)=>{if(!v||typeof v!=='object'||seen.has(v))return;seen.add(v);
      if(v.deviceMediaKey&&typeof v.src==='string'&&v.src.includes('ethvelle-device-media://')){
        const wrapped=/^url\(/i.test(v.src),key=String(v.deviceMediaKey);
        tasks.push((async()=>{try{
          const rec=await getSourceMedia(key);if(!rec)throw new Error('Saved media is missing');let mediaBlob=rec.blob||null;
          if(!mediaBlob&&rec.handle&&typeof rec.handle.getFile==='function'){
            if(typeof rec.handle.queryPermission==='function'){
              const permission=await rec.handle.queryPermission({mode:'read'});
              if(permission!=='granted')throw new Error('The browser needs renewed permission for this device file. Re-select it from the device picker.');
            }
            mediaBlob=await rec.handle.getFile();
            // Cache a durable Blob copy now so later refreshes do not depend on the original path.
            try{await putSourceMedia(key,mediaBlob,{name:rec.name,type:rec.type,lastModified:rec.lastModified});}
            catch(cacheErr){console.warn('Ethvelle: legacy media could not be upgraded to Blob storage',key,cacheErr);}
          }
          if(!mediaBlob)throw new Error('Saved media is missing');
          const url=URL.createObjectURL(mediaBlob);v.src=wrapped?'url('+url+')':url;delete v.__evDeviceMediaMissing;
        }catch(e){v.__evDeviceMediaMissing=true;console.warn('Ethvelle: could not restore device media',key,e);if(!window.__evDeviceMediaWarningShown){window.__evDeviceMediaWarningShown=true;setTimeout(()=>toast('A media file needs renewed device-file permission. Re-select that file if it appears missing.'),900);}}})());
      }
      if(Array.isArray(v))v.forEach(walk);else Object.keys(v).forEach(k=>walk(v[k]));
    };
    walk(root);await Promise.all(tasks);
  }
  function toast(msg){try{if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(msg,{duration:2600});}catch(_e){}}
  function openProjectDb(){
    if(!('indexedDB' in window))return Promise.reject(new Error('This browser does not support IndexedDB.'));
    if(!dbPromise){
      dbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const req=indexedDB.open(DB_NAME,1);
        req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{const db=req.result;if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('snapshots'))db.createObjectStore('snapshots',{keyPath:'key'});};
        req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not open device storage.'));
        req.onblocked=()=>reject(new Error('Device storage is busy in another tab.'));
      }).catch(err=>{dbPromise=null;throw err;});
    }
    return dbPromise;
  }
  function approxJsonSize(root,limit){
    let total=0,stack=[root],seen=0;
    while(stack.length){const v=stack.pop();if(v==null)continue;const t=typeof v;
      if(t==='string'){total+=v.length+2;}
      else if(t==='number'||t==='boolean'){total+=16;}
      else if(t==='object'){
        if(++seen>150000)return limit+1;
        if(Array.isArray(v)){total+=2;for(let i=0;i<v.length;i++)stack.push(v[i]);}
        else{total+=2;for(const k of Object.keys(v)){total+=k.length+4;stack.push(v[k]);}}
      }
      if(total>limit)return total;
    }
    return total;
  }
  function makeProjectIndex(){
    try{return projects.map(p=>({id:p.id,name:p.name,ratio:p.ratio,created:p.created,duration:p.duration,updatedAt:p.updatedAt||0,thumbnail:(typeof p.thumbnail==='string'&&!/^data:/i.test(p.thumbnail))?p.thumbnail:undefined,savedState:null,__evIndexOnly:true}));}
    catch(_e){return [];}
  }
  let deviceSnapshotQueue=Promise.resolve();
  async function saveFullProjectsToDevice(snapshotOverride){
    const snapshot=snapshotOverride||projectSnapshotWithMediaRefs();
    const task=deviceSnapshotQueue.catch(()=>{}).then(async()=>{
      const db=await openProjectDb();
      await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
        const tx=db.transaction('snapshots','readwrite');
        tx.objectStore('snapshots').put({key:'all-projects',projects:snapshot,savedAt:Date.now()});
        tx.oncomplete=resolve;tx.onerror=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Project save failed.'));tx.onabort=()=>reject(tx.error||new Error('Project save was interrupted.'));
      });
      return true;
    });
    deviceSnapshotQueue=task;
    return task;
  }
  window.__ethvelleDeviceSaveNow=()=>saveFullProjectsToDevice();
  function queueDeviceSave(delay){
    clearTimeout(saveTimer);
    saveTimer=setTimeout(()=>{saveTimer=0;saveFullProjectsToDevice().catch(err=>{
      console.error('Ethvelle project storage:',err);
      const now=Date.now();if(now-lastStorageErrorAt>10000){lastStorageErrorAt=now;toast('Could not save all project data to this device. Export a backup before leaving.');}
    });},Number.isFinite(delay)?delay:250);
  }
  // Keep small projects in localStorage for quick startup; keep big video/image projects in IndexedDB
  // so a few large Base64 media files cannot exhaust the usual localStorage quota.
  let localIndexGeneration=0;
  window.saveProjectsToStorage=function(){
    let size=0;try{size=approxJsonSize(projects,1400000);}catch(_e){size=1400001;}
    // Every save supersedes previous asynchronous index publications, including a transition
    // from a large/device-media project back to the small localStorage branch.
    const generation=++localIndexGeneration;
    let hasDeviceMedia=false;
    try{hasDeviceMedia=(projects||[]).some(p=>{const st=p&&p.savedState;if(!st)return false;return [...(Array.isArray(st.overlays)?st.overlays:[]),...(Array.isArray(st.backgrounds)?st.backgrounds:[])].some(m=>m&&m.deviceMediaKey);});}catch(_e){}
    if(size<=1400000&&!hasDeviceMedia){
      try{localStorage.setItem('riggy.projects.v1',JSON.stringify(projects));}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle local project save:',err);}
      queueDeviceSave(240);
      return true;
    }
    const snapshot=projectSnapshotWithMediaRefs();
    return saveFullProjectsToDevice(snapshot).then(()=>{
      if(generation===localIndexGeneration)localStorage.setItem('riggy.projects.v1',JSON.stringify(makeProjectIndex()));
      return true;
    }).catch(err=>{
      console.error('Ethvelle durable project save failed:',err);
      const now=Date.now();if(now-lastStorageErrorAt>10000){lastStorageErrorAt=now;toast('Could not finish saving this project to device storage. Keep the editor open and export a backup before leaving.');}
      return false;
    });
  };
  async function restoreProjectsFromDevice(){
    try{
      const db=await openProjectDb();
      const rec=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const tx=db.transaction('snapshots','readonly'),req=tx.objectStore('snapshots').get('all-projects');req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result||null);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error||new Error('Could not read stored projects.'));});
      if(!rec||!Array.isArray(rec.projects))return;
      const device=rec.projects;
      let sourceMediaRecords=[];
      try{sourceMediaRecords=await listSourceMediaRecords();}catch(mediaErr){console.warn('Ethvelle media recovery lookup:',mediaErr);}
      const repairedDevice=repairLegacyOverlayMediaRefs(device,sourceMediaRecords);
      await rehydrateDeviceMediaRefs(device);
      const local=Array.isArray(projects)?projects:[],byId=new Map();
      device.forEach(p=>{if(p&&p.id!=null)byId.set(String(p.id),p);});
      local.forEach(p=>{
        if(!p||p.id==null)return;const id=String(p.id),saved=byId.get(id);
        if(!saved||(!p.__evIndexOnly&&Number(p.updatedAt||0)>Number(saved.updatedAt||0)))byId.set(id,p);
      });
      projects=Array.from(byId.values()).map(p=>{if(p&&p.__evIndexOnly){const q={...p};delete q.__evIndexOnly;return q;}return p;});
      const repairedMerged=repairLegacyOverlayMediaRefs(projects,sourceMediaRecords);
      await rehydrateDeviceMediaRefs(projects);
      try{await Promise.race([window.__evFramesGate,new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,5000))]);if(window.__evSourceFrames)await window.__evSourceFrames.restoreForProjects(projects);}catch(frameErr){console.warn('Ethvelle frame restore:',frameErr);}
      projects.sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.created||0)-new Date(a.created||0));
      // Make repaired references durable so the next refresh does not have to rediscover them.
      if(repairedDevice||repairedMerged){
        try{window.saveProjectsToStorage();await saveFullProjectsToDevice();}catch(saveErr){console.warn('Ethvelle repaired-media snapshot:',saveErr);}
      }
      try{if(typeof window.renderProjects==='function')window.renderProjects();if(typeof window.updateProfileUI==='function')window.updateProfileUI();}catch(_e){}
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: stored project recovery unavailable:',err);}
  }
  // Promote the storage request immediately from the user's click, then open the device DB.
  async function requestStorageAndContinue(){
    const allow=$('evspAllow'),backdrop=$('ethvelleStoragePermission');if(allow){allow.disabled=true;allow.textContent='Preparing storage…';}
    let persistenceGranted=null;
    try{if(navigator.storage&&typeof navigator.storage.persist==='function'){try{persistenceGranted=await navigator.storage.persist();}catch(_e){persistenceGranted=false;}}}catch(_e){persistenceGranted=false;}
    try{await openProjectDb();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: IndexedDB could not be opened:',err);}
    try{localStorage.setItem(CONSENT_KEY,'yes');}catch(_e){}
    backdrop?.classList.remove('active');if(allow){allow.disabled=false;allow.textContent='Allow & continue';}
    if(persistenceGranted===false)toast('Browser storage is available, but permanent retention was not guaranteed. Export important projects as backups.');
    if(pendingCreate){pendingCreate=false;try{if(originalCreateProject)originalCreateProject.call(window);if(projects&&projects[0])projects[0].updatedAt=Date.now();window.saveProjectsToStorage();}catch(err){console.error(err);toast('Project could not be created. Please try again.');}}
  }
  const modal=$('ethvelleStoragePermission');
  $('evspAllow')?.addEventListener('click',requestStorageAndContinue);
  $('evspCancel')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{pendingCreate=false;modal?.classList.remove('active');});
  modal?.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===modal){pendingCreate=false;modal.classList.remove('active');}});
  const originalCreateProject=window.createProject;
  if(typeof originalCreateProject==='function'&&!originalCreateProject.__evStorageGate){
    const gatedCreateProject=function(){
      let consent=false;try{consent=localStorage.getItem(CONSENT_KEY)==='yes';}catch(_e){}
      /* v219-fix: no permission panel any more. Storage is requested automatically in the background
         (persistent-storage request + opening the device DB) and the project is created straight away. */
      if(!consent){
        try{localStorage.setItem(CONSENT_KEY,'yes');}catch(_e){}
        try{if(navigator.storage&&typeof navigator.storage.persist==='function'){Promise.resolve(navigator.storage.persist()).catch(()=>{});}}catch(_e){}
        try{Promise.resolve(openProjectDb()).catch(err=>console.warn('Ethvelle: IndexedDB could not be opened:',err));}catch(_e){}
      }
      const result=originalCreateProject.apply(this,arguments);
      try{if(projects&&projects[0])projects[0].updatedAt=Date.now();window.saveProjectsToStorage();}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    gatedCreateProject.__evStorageGate=true;window.createProject=gatedCreateProject;
  }
  const originalPersist=window.persistProjectState;
  if(typeof originalPersist==='function'&&!originalPersist.__evDeviceSave){
    const wrappedPersist=function(){try{if(typeof currentProject!=='undefined'&&currentProject)currentProject.updatedAt=Date.now();}catch(_e){}return originalPersist.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrappedPersist.__evDeviceSave=true;window.persistProjectState=wrappedPersist;
  }
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=0;saveFullProjectsToDevice().catch(()=>{});});
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.visibilityState==='hidden'){clearTimeout(saveTimer);saveTimer=0;saveFullProjectsToDevice().catch(()=>{});}});
  // Opening a project before the IndexedDB restore finishes used to pass the early
  // localStorage index-only record (savedState:null) into openEditor. That made every Clips
  // row appear empty until the user re-uploaded media, even though the full snapshot existed.
  // Publish a restore promise and let the final openEditor guard wait for it.
  window.__evFramesGate = new Promise(r=>{window.__evFramesGateResolve=r;});
  window.__ethvelleProjectsRestoreReady = false;
  window.__ethvelleProjectsRestorePromise = Promise.resolve()
    .then(()=>restoreProjectsFromDevice())
    .catch(err=>{console.warn('Ethvelle project restore:',err);})
    .finally(()=>{window.__ethvelleProjectsRestoreReady = true;});

  // Video thumbnails are generated only by the time-windowed sequential decoder in V9.
  // The compatibility generateVideoThumbnails() helper above resolves metadata only.

  // Legacy per-tile live-video fallback removed; video rows use the shared lazy tile decoder.

  // Compare the selected media directly on the main canvas. Only the white wipe/handle accepts
  // pointer input; the comparison slider in the settings sheet is deliberately removed.
  function unwrappedSource(src){let s=String(src||'').trim(),m=s.match(/^url\((.*)\)$/i);if(m)s=m[1].trim().replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g,'');return s;}
  function getEnhanceTarget(){
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
    try{const bg=(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'?backgrounds:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===id);if(bg&&unwrappedSource(bg.src))return {kind:'background',model:bg,type:'image',src:unwrappedSource(bg.src)};}catch(_e){}
    try{const ov=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'?overlays:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===id);if(ov&&['video','image'].includes(ov.type)&&unwrappedSource(ov.src))return {kind:'overlay',model:ov,type:ov.type,src:unwrappedSource(ov.src)};}catch(_e){}
    if(!id){try{const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec==='number'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0):null;if(bg&&unwrappedSource(bg.src))return {kind:'background',model:bg,type:'image',src:unwrappedSource(bg.src)};}catch(_e){}}
    return null;
  }
  function transformFor(target){
    const t=target.kind==='background'?(target.model.transform||((typeof bgTransform!=='undefined')?bgTransform:null)):target.model.transform;
    if(!t)return 'none';return `translate(${Number(t.offsetX)||0}px,${Number(t.offsetY)||0}px) rotate(${Number(t.rotation)||0}deg) scale(${Number(t.scale)||1})`;
  }
  function referenceMediaForCompare(target){
    try{
      if(target.kind==='background')return $('bgImgLayer');
      const host=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[target.model.id]:null;
      if(!host)return null;
      if(host.matches&&host.matches('video,img,canvas'))return host;
      return host.querySelector?host.querySelector('video,img,canvas'):host;
    }catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  let comparePipeline=null,comparePipelineKey='',compareLastPlaybackSyncAt=0;
  let compareSnap=null,compareSnapCtx=null,compareLastSource=null,compareLastKey='',compareLastTime=-1,compareLastCost=0,compareRvfcEl=null,compareRvfcId=0,compareLoopTimer=0;
  const COMPARE_PREVIEW_LONG_SIDE=720;
  function makeCompareMedia(target,after){
    const el=document.createElement('canvas');el.className='evcc-media';el.width=2;el.height=2;el.dataset.evSide=after?'after':'before';
    el.style.width='100%';el.style.height='100%';el.style.objectFit='contain';el.style.objectPosition='center';el.style.transform=transformFor(target);el.style.transformOrigin='center center';el.draggable=false;
    const reference=referenceMediaForCompare(target);if(reference){try{const cs=getComputedStyle(reference);if(cs.objectFit)el.style.objectFit=cs.objectFit;if(cs.objectPosition)el.style.objectPosition=cs.objectPosition;if(cs.transform&&cs.transform!=='none')el.style.transform=cs.transform;if(cs.transformOrigin)el.style.transformOrigin=cs.transformOrigin;}catch(_e){}}
    return el;
  }
  /* The tool panel's full-screen click-catcher (#panelBackdrop, z-index 100100) sits above the
     preview and swallowed every touch meant for the divider. Cut a hole in it exactly over the
     divider so the handle can be dragged while taps elsewhere still behave as before. */
  function updateBackdropHole(){
    const bd=document.getElementById('panelBackdrop');if(!bd)return;
    const wipe=document.querySelector('#ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot .evcc-wipe');
    if(!wipe){bd.style.removeProperty('clip-path');return;}
    const b=bd.getBoundingClientRect(),w0=wipe.getBoundingClientRect(),rr=wipe.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();if(!b.width||!w0.width)return;
    const l=Math.max(w0.left,rr.left),r=Math.min(w0.right,rr.right),w={left:l,top:w0.top,width:Math.max(0,r-l),height:w0.height};if(!w.width)return;
    const x=Math.round(w.left-b.left),y=Math.round(w.top-b.top),ww=Math.round(w.width),hh=Math.round(w.height),W=Math.round(b.width),H=Math.round(b.height);
    bd.style.setProperty('clip-path',`path(evenodd,'M0 0H${W}V${H}H0Z M${x} ${y}h${ww}v${hh}h-${ww}Z')`,'important');
  }
  window.__evUpdateBackdropHole=updateBackdropHole;
  function setCanvasComparePosition(p){
    canvasComparePercent=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(p)||0));const root=$('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot');if(!root)return;
    const after=root.querySelector('.evcc-after'),wipe=root.querySelector('.evcc-wipe');if(after)after.style.clipPath=`inset(0 0 0 ${canvasComparePercent}%)`;
    if(wipe){const width=root.clientWidth||root.getBoundingClientRect().width||0,x=width*canvasComparePercent/100;wipe.style.left=x+'px';wipe.style.setProperty('--evcc-line-offset','31px');updateBackdropHole();}
  }
  /* v287 BEFORE/AFTER SYNC FIX. The two halves used to be filled by two separate reads of the live video
     (the AFTER half through a full-resolution WebGL pass that was also rebuilt every time playback started or
     stopped), so AFTER could trail BEFORE by 0.1-0.5 s. Now:
       1. ONE snapshot of the current video frame is taken per update;
       2. BEFORE and AFTER are both drawn from that same snapshot in the same synchronous block, so they can never
          show different moments;
       3. updates are driven by the video's own frame callback (requestVideoFrameCallback) plus seeked/loadeddata,
          so both halves advance on exactly the frames the video presents;
       4. the preview pipeline works on the small snapshot and is NOT rebuilt on play/pause, so it is cheap and
          never stalls the start of playback. */
  function syncCanvasCompare(force){
    force=(force===true);
    const root=$('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot');if(!root)return;
    const target=getEnhanceTarget();if(!target)return;
    const before=root.querySelector('.evcc-before .evcc-media'),after=root.querySelector('.evcc-after .evcc-media');if(!before||!after)return;
    const source=referenceMediaForCompare(target);if(!source)return;
    const isVideo=source.tagName==='VIDEO';
    const sw=Number(source.videoWidth||source.naturalWidth||source.width)||0,sh=Number(source.videoHeight||source.naturalHeight||source.height)||0;
    if(!sw||!sh||(isVideo&&source.readyState<2))return;
    const live=!!(isPlaying||window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing);
    const now=performance.now();
    if(live&&!force){
      // While playing, the video frame callback drives the updates; only fall back to the render loop when it is unavailable.
      if(isVideo&&compareRvfcEl===source&&typeof source.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function')return;
      if(compareLastPlaybackSyncAt&&now-compareLastPlaybackSyncAt<33)return;
    }
    const factor=Math.min(1,COMPARE_PREVIEW_LONG_SIDE/Math.max(sw,sh)),pw=Math.max(2,Math.round(sw*factor)),ph=Math.max(2,Math.round(sh*factor));
    const amount=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number($('evltEnhanceAmount')?.value??target.model.enhanceAmount??0)||0));
    const filter=typeof window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter==='function'?window.ethvelleAdjustmentFilter(target.model.adjustments,0):'none';
    const key=`${source.currentSrc||source.src||''}|${pw}x${ph}|${amount}|${filter}`,t=isVideo?Number(source.currentTime)||0:0;
    if(!live&&!force&&source===compareLastSource&&key===compareLastKey&&t===compareLastTime)return;
    if(!isVideo&&!force&&source===compareLastSource&&key===compareLastKey)return;
    const started=performance.now();
    // 1) ONE snapshot of the current frame - both halves are built from this and nothing else.
    if(!compareSnap)compareSnap=document.createElement('canvas');
    if(compareSnap.width!==pw||compareSnap.height!==ph){compareSnap.width=pw;compareSnap.height=ph;compareSnapCtx=null;}
    if(!compareSnapCtx){compareSnapCtx=compareSnap.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});if(!compareSnapCtx)return;compareSnapCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;compareSnapCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';}
    try{compareSnapCtx.drawImage(source,0,0,pw,ph);}catch(_e){return;}
    // 2) Preview pipeline sized to the snapshot (rebuilt only if the preview size itself changes).
    const pkey=pw+'x'+ph;
    if(comparePipelineKey!==pkey||!comparePipeline){
      try{if(comparePipeline)comparePipeline.dispose();comparePipeline=window.ethvelleCreateEnhancePipeline(pw,ph,pw,ph);comparePipelineKey=pkey;}
      catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: comparison processor unavailable',err);comparePipeline=null;comparePipelineKey='';}
    }
    let processed=null;
    if(comparePipeline){try{processed=comparePipeline.render(compareSnap,filter,amount);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle: comparison frame failed',err);}}
    // 3) Paint BEFORE and AFTER back-to-back in the same synchronous block, from the same snapshot.
    if(before.width!==pw||before.height!==ph){before.width=pw;before.height=ph;}
    if(after.width!==pw||after.height!==ph){after.width=pw;after.height=ph;}
    const beforeCtx=before.getContext('2d'),afterCtx=after.getContext('2d');
    if(beforeCtx){beforeCtx.save();beforeCtx.filter=filter;beforeCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;beforeCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';beforeCtx.clearRect(0,0,pw,ph);beforeCtx.drawImage(compareSnap,0,0,pw,ph);beforeCtx.restore();}
    if(afterCtx&&processed){afterCtx.clearRect(0,0,pw,ph);afterCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;afterCtx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';afterCtx.drawImage(processed,0,0,pw,ph);}
    if(!live)setCanvasComparePosition(canvasComparePercent);
    compareLastSource=source;compareLastKey=key;compareLastTime=t;compareLastPlaybackSyncAt=performance.now();compareLastCost=compareLastPlaybackSyncAt-started;
  }
  const compareFrameHandler=()=>{try{syncCanvasCompare(true);}catch(_e){}};
  function detachCompareFrameLoop(){
    if(compareLoopTimer){clearInterval(compareLoopTimer);compareLoopTimer=0;}
    if(compareRvfcEl){
      try{if(compareRvfcId&&typeof compareRvfcEl.cancelVideoFrameCallback==='function')compareRvfcEl.cancelVideoFrameCallback(compareRvfcId);}catch(_e){}

    }
    compareRvfcEl=null;compareRvfcId=0;
  }
  function attachCompareFrameLoop(){
    detachCompareFrameLoop();
    const check=()=>{
      if(!$('ethvelleCanvasCompareRoot')){detachCompareFrameLoop();return;}
      const tg=getEnhanceTarget(),src=tg?referenceMediaForCompare(tg):null;
      if(src===compareRvfcEl)return;
      if(compareRvfcEl){

        compareRvfcEl=src;
        src.addEventListener('seeked',compareFrameHandler);src.addEventListener('loadeddata',compareFrameHandler);src.addEventListener('pause',compareFrameHandler);
        if(typeof src.requestVideoFrameCallback==='function'){
          const onFrame=()=>{

        }
      }
    };

  }

  function mountCanvasCompare(){

    const updateFromEvent=e=>{const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();if(r.width)setCanvasComparePosition((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width*100);};

    });
    wipe.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(canvasCompareDrag&&canvasCompareDrag.id===e.pointerId){

      canvasCompareDrag.moved=true;updateFromEvent(e);e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    }});

    wipe.addEventListener('pointerup',end);wipe.addEventListener('pointercancel',end);
    wipe.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='ArrowLeft'){setCanvasComparePosition(canvasComparePercent-2);e.preventDefault();}else if(e.key==='ArrowRight'){setCanvasComparePosition(canvasComparePercent+2);e.preventDefault();}});
    canvasComparePercent=50;setCanvasComparePosition(50);

  }
  function renderEnhanceSettings(){

  }
  /* v294: first time the panel opens, show a loading state while the device comfort test runs (see __evEnhanceCaps). */
  function applyEnhanceCapsUi(){

    const start=$('evltEnhanceStart'),sel=$('evltEnhanceQuality');

      if(ov)ov.remove();settings.classList.remove('evlt-caps-busy');
      if(sel)sel.disabled=false;if(start&&!window.__ethvelleV5EnhanceBusy)start.disabled=false;
      return;
    }
    settings.classList.add('evlt-caps-busy');
    if(!ov){ov=document.createElement('div');ov.className='evlt-caps-loading';ov.setAttribute('role','status');ov.innerHTML='<i class="evlt-caps-spin"></i><b>Checking your device…</b><span>Finding the best Enhance quality for this phone</span>';settings.appendChild(ov);}
    if(sel)sel.disabled=true;if(start)start.disabled=true;
    try{ensure().then(()=>applyEnhanceCapsUi(),()=>applyEnhanceCapsUi());}catch(_e){}
  }
  const oldSetEnhance=window.ethvelleSetEnhanceAmount;
  if(typeof oldSetEnhance==='function'&&!oldSetEnhance.__evCanvasCompare){const w=function(v){const r=oldSetEnhance.apply(this,arguments);syncCanvasCompare();return r;};w.__evCanvasCompare=true;window.ethvelleSetEnhanceAmount=w;}
  const oldStartEnhance=window.ethvelleStartVideoEnhance;
